### [1] [The Ellis and Baldwin test of the Cosmic Dipole: Exploring the impact of multiple flux density cuts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27520)
*Vasudev Mittal,Geraint F. Lewis*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: This paper proposes a novel method to measure the cosmic matter dipole by incorporating the full source luminosity function (LF) through multi-bin flux analysis, improving statistical consistency and model comparison over traditional single-flux-cut approaches. This yields a higher Bayes factor for non-power-law LFs and enhances sensitivity to the cosmic dipole tension, especially when applied to upcoming radio and infrared surveys.
\n\n
本文提出了一种新颖的方法，通过多流量区间分析将完整的源光度函数（Luminosity Function, LF）纳入宇宙物质偶极测量中，相比传统的单一流量截断方法，在统计一致性和模型比较方面有所改进。该方法对非幂律型LF给出更高的贝叶斯因子，增强了对宇宙偶极张力的敏感性，尤其适用于未来的射电和红外巡天。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The cosmic dipole tension—discrepancy between the CMB kinematic dipole and the matter dipole from galaxy surveys—challenges the Cosmological Principle. Traditional methods estimate the matter dipole using a single flux threshold, discarding valuable information encoded in the shape of the luminosity function (LF). This loss limits both statistical power and physical insight, motivating a method that fully exploits the LF’s structure.
\n\n
宇宙偶极张力——即宇宙微波背景（CMB）运动学偶极与星系巡天推断出的物质偶极之间的不一致——对宇宙学原理构成挑战。传统方法使用单一通量阈值估计物质偶极，忽略了光度函数（Luminosity Function, LF）形状中蕴含的重要信息，从而限制了统计效力和物理洞察力，因此亟需一种能充分利用LF结构的新方法。

Method: The authors divide the source catalogue into disjoint flux bins and perform a joint Bayesian fit of the matter dipole amplitude and direction across all bins, explicitly modeling the underlying luminosity function. This approach retains information about the LF’s shape—especially deviations from a power law—and leverages variations in source counts across flux thresholds. The method is optimized by placing bin edges where the LF exhibits significant curvature or breaks.
\n\n
作者将源星表划分为互不重叠的流量区间，并对所有区间联合进行贝叶斯拟合，同时拟合物质偶极的幅度和方向，并显式建模底层光度函数（LF）。该方法保留了LF形状的信息——特别是对幂律偏离的部分——并利用不同流量阈值下源计数的变化。通过在LF曲率显著或存在拐点处设置区间边界，可优化该方法性能。

Result: For luminosity functions that deviate from a simple power law, the multi-bin method yields a higher Bayes factor compared to the standard single-threshold approach, indicating superior model evidence. The gain is maximized when flux bins are chosen in regions where the LF changes rapidly. The technique is shown to be feasible for next-generation all-sky surveys at radio and infrared wavelengths, with potential to resolve or sharpen the cosmic dipole tension.
\n\n
对于偏离简单幂律的光度函数，多区间方法相比标准单阈值方法获得了更高的贝叶斯因子，表明其具有更优的模型证据。当流量区间选在LF快速变化的区域时，增益最大。该技术被证明适用于下一代全天空射电和红外观测巡天，有望解决或进一步明确宇宙偶极张力问题。

Conclusion: Integrating the full luminosity function into matter dipole estimation significantly improves the robustness and informativeness of the measurement. This new framework not only addresses a key limitation of conventional methods but also opens a path toward decisive tests of large-scale isotropy with upcoming surveys, potentially reshaping our understanding of fundamental cosmological symmetries.
\n\n
将完整光度函数纳入物质偶极测量显著提升了结果的稳健性和信息量。这一新框架不仅克服了传统方法的关键局限，还为利用未来巡天对大尺度各向同性进行决定性检验开辟了路径，可能重塑我们对基本宇宙学对称性的理解。

Abstract: The cosmic dipole tension - the discrepancy between the Cosmic Microwave Background kinematic dipole and the matter dipole inferred from all-sky surveys poses a significant challenge to the Cosmological Principle, which dictates that the universe is homogeneous and isotropic at the largest scales. Traditional measurement of the matter dipole requires selecting an appropriate limiting flux and calculating the dipolar modulation using sources brighter than the flux. This approach, however, ignores the shape of the source luminosity function (LF) and deprives the analysis of this crucial information. In this study, we present a new approach to calculate the matter dipole by integrating the source flux distribution into the analysis. We achieve this by dividing the catalogue into disjoint flux bins and simultaneously fitting the matter dipole across them. For non-power-law LFs, this method gives a higher Bayes factor - and hence a better description of the matter dipole - as compared to the traditional approach. The method works best when the flux cuts are selected in regions where the LF's shape changes significantly. We discuss the feasibility of this method for upcoming cosmological surveys and show that it has the potential to yield decisive results at both radio and infrared wavelengths.

</details>


### [2] [GINKAKU: Scalable Cosmological Structure Formation Simulation Code and Post-processing Pipeline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28581)
*Takahiro Nishimichi,Satoshi Tanaka,Kohji Yoshikawa*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: GINKAKU is a new cosmological $N$-body code built for the Dark Quest II project, enabling high-fidelity simulations across diverse cosmologies—including massive neutrinos and clustering dark energy—by combining TreePM gravity with linear-response treatments of non-particle components in the $N$-body gauge. It achieves sub-1% code-to-code agreement with established codes and supports emulator-ready production runs.
\n\n
GINKAKU 是为 Dark Quest II 项目开发的新一代宇宙学 $N$-body 代码，通过在 $N$-body gauge 中结合 TreePM 引力求解器与非粒子成分（如大质量中微子和成团暗能量）的线性响应处理，实现对多种宇宙学模型的高保真模拟。该代码与主流代码的差异可控制在 1% 以内，并支持用于构建 emulator 的大规模生产运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Next-generation galaxy surveys demand precise theoretical predictions across extended cosmological parameter spaces, including non-standard ingredients like massive neutrinos and clustering dark energy with non-unit effective sound speed. Existing $N$-body codes often lack consistent integration of general-relativistic effects, radiation, and external source terms while maintaining Newtonian dynamics on small scales. GINKAKU addresses this gap by providing a unified framework that incorporates these components at the linear level without compromising subhorizon particle evolution.
\n\n
下一代星系巡天要求在扩展的宇宙学参数空间（包括大质量中微子和具有非单位有效声速的成团暗能量等非标准成分）内提供精确的理论预言。现有 $N$-body 代码通常难以在保持小尺度牛顿动力学的同时，一致地整合广义相对论效应、辐射及外部源项。GINKAKU 通过提供一个统一框架，在线性层次上纳入这些成分，同时不破坏次视界尺度上的粒子演化，从而填补这一空白。

Method: GINKAKU is built on the FDPS framework and employs a TreePM gravity solver. Non-particle components (massive neutrinos, dark energy, radiation) are treated via linear-response theory in the $N$-body gauge, where their perturbations act as external source terms in the Poisson equation. This preserves Newtonian equations of motion for CDM+baryon particles on subhorizon scales while consistently including relativistic and early-universe effects. The code is validated through convergence tests and cross-comparisons with GADGET, PKDGRAV3, and RAMSES using identical initial conditions. A post-processing pipeline computes halo properties, including mass function and shapes, with inter-resolution scatter reduced to $\sim1\%$.
\n\n
GINKAKU 基于 FDPS 框架，采用 TreePM 引力求解器。非粒子成分（大质量中微子、暗能量、辐射）通过 $N$-body gauge 中的线性响应理论处理，其扰动作为泊松方程中的外部源项。该方法在次视界尺度上保持 CDM+重子粒子的牛顿运动方程，同时自洽地包含相对论效应和早期宇宙效应。代码通过收敛性测试及与 GADGET、PKDGRAV3 和 RAMSES 在相同初始条件下的交叉比对进行验证。后处理流程计算晕的质量函数和形状等性质，不同分辨率间的弥散被控制在 $\sim1\%$。

Result: GINKAKU achieves code-to-code power spectrum differences below $\sim1\%$ when internal accuracy parameters are tuned, with a fiducial setting identified for production runs. Applied to eight DQ2 cosmologies with $3,000^3$ particles in up to $4\,h^{-1}\mathrm{Gpc}$ boxes, it reproduces expected nonlinear signatures of massive neutrinos and clustering dark energy. The post-processing pipeline yields a halo mass function with $\sim1\%$ inter-resolution spread and provides halo shape data for intrinsic alignment studies. These simulations underpin a total matter power spectrum emulator presented in a companion paper.
\n\n
GINKAKU 在调整内部精度参数后，可将与其他代码的功率谱差异控制在 $\sim1\%$ 以下，并确定了一组适用于生产运行的基准参数设置。在八个 DQ2 宇宙学模型（$3,000^3$ 粒子，盒子边长最大达 $4\,h^{-1}\mathrm{Gpc}$）上的应用成功复现了大质量中微子和成团暗能量预期的非线性特征。后处理流程得到的晕质量函数在不同分辨率间的弥散约为 $\sim1\%$，并提供了用于本征取向（intrinsic alignment）研究的晕形状数据。这些模拟支撑了伴随论文中提出的总物质功率谱 emulator。

Conclusion: GINKAKU provides a robust, accurate, and scalable platform for ensemble $N$-body simulations across extended cosmological models required by Stage-IV surveys. Its consistent treatment of linear relativistic and non-CDM components within a Newtonian particle framework enables controlled emulation of nonlinear structure formation, making it suitable for precision cosmology with massive neutrinos, clustering dark energy, and beyond-$\Lambda$CDM physics.
\n\n
GINKAKU 为 Stage-IV 巡天所需的扩展宇宙学模型提供了稳健、精确且可扩展的 $N$-body 模拟平台。其在牛顿粒子框架内对线性相对论效应及非 CDM 成分的一致处理，使得非线性结构形成的可控 emulated 成为可能，适用于包含大质量中微子、成团暗能量及其他超出 $\Lambda$CDM 物理的精密宇宙学研究。

Abstract: We introduce GINKAKU, a new cosmological $N$-body code developed for the Dark Quest II (DQ2) simulation campaign and designed for controlled ensemble production across the cosmological model space required by next-generation galaxy surveys, including massive neutrinos and clustering dark energy. Built on the FDPS framework, GINKAKU couples a TreePM gravity solver with a linear-response treatment of external source terms for components not evolved as $N$-body particles, formulated in the $N$-body gauge. This design incorporates massive-neutrino perturbations, general-relativistic corrections, early-time radiation perturbations, and dark-energy clustering with non-unit effective sound speed at the linear level, while preserving Newtonian particle dynamics on subhorizon scales. The code is validated through internal convergence studies and cross-comparisons with GADGET, PKDGRAV3, and RAMSES on shared initial conditions: code-to-code differences in the nonlinear power spectrum can be reduced below $\sim1\%$ level by tuning internal accuracy parameters, and we identify a production-grade fiducial setting achieving this control at modest cost. We apply GINKAKU to an initial set of DQ2 production runs -- eight cosmological models with $3,000^3$ particles in boxes up to $4\,h^{-1}\mathrm{Gpc}$ -- processed by a renewed post-processing pipeline that reduces the inter-resolution spread of the halo mass function to $\sim 1\%$ and includes halo-shape measurements for intrinsic-alignment statistics. The scale-dependent-growth cosmologies reproduce the expected nonlinear signatures of massive neutrinos and clustering dark energy, demonstrating suitability for emulator-scale production. A total matter power spectrum emulator from these runs is presented in an accompanying paper. (abridged)

</details>


### [3] [Dark Quest II: A Wide-Coverage Neural Network Emulator of the Nonlinear Matter Power Spectrum Across Extended Cosmologies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28596)
*Satoshi Tanaka,Takahiro Nishimichi,Yosuke Kobayashi*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: DarkEmulator2 is a neural network-based emulator for the nonlinear matter power spectrum across a 9D $w_0 w_a \nu$CDM cosmological parameter space, achieving subpercent accuracy up to $k \simeq 10\,h\,\mathrm{Mpc}^{-1}$ by leveraging multi-resolution N-body simulations and physically motivated auxiliary inputs.
\n\n
DarkEmulator2 是一个基于神经网络的非线性物质功率谱模拟器，覆盖九维 $w_0 w_a \nu$CDM 宇宙学参数空间，通过利用多分辨率 N-body 模拟和物理动机明确的辅助输入，在高达 $k \simeq 10\,h\,\mathrm{Mpc}^{-1}$ 的尺度上实现亚百分比精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The paper aims to construct a high-precision, generalizable emulator for the nonlinear matter power spectrum in extended cosmological models ($w_0 w_a \nu$CDM), addressing limitations of existing emulators that either lack coverage in neutrino or dark energy parameters or rely on fixed-resolution simulations. The motivation includes enabling robust inference for next-generation large-scale structure (LSS) surveys by providing accurate theoretical predictions deep into the nonlinear regime.
\n\n
本文旨在构建一个高精度、可泛化的非线性物质功率谱模拟器，适用于扩展宇宙学模型（$w_0 w_a \nu$CDM），以解决现有模拟器在中微子或暗能量参数空间覆盖不足、或依赖固定分辨率模拟的局限性。其动机包括为下一代大尺度结构（LSS）巡天提供深入非线性区间的精确理论预言，从而支持稳健的参数推断。

Method: The authors train a single neural network on N-body simulations from three resolution tiers generated by the Ginkaku code. The input to the network includes: (i) the 9D cosmological parameter vector, (ii) the linear matter power spectrum $P_{\mathrm{lin}}(k)$, (iii) simulation resolution descriptors (e.g., particle mass, force resolution), and (iv) a low-dimensional summary (e.g., principal components) of the initial Gaussian random field. This unified input strategy enhances interpolation and extrapolation across parameter space. The training leverages high-resolution simulations sparsely while using abundant lower-resolution runs for broad coverage. The emulator targets boxes of size $L_{\mathrm{box}} = 1\,h^{-1}\mathrm{Gpc}$ with up to $3000^3$ particles.
\n\n
作者使用 Ginkaku 代码生成的三个分辨率层级的 N-body 模拟训练单一神经网络。网络输入包括：(i) 九维宇宙学参数向量，(ii) 线性物质功率谱 $P_{\mathrm{lin}}(k)$，(iii) 模拟分辨率描述符（如粒子质量、力分辨率），以及 (iv) 初始高斯随机场的低维摘要（如主成分）。这种统一输入策略增强了参数空间中的插值与外推能力。训练策略稀疏地使用高分辨率模拟，同时利用大量低分辨率模拟实现广泛覆盖。模拟器针对 $L_{\mathrm{box}} = 1\,h^{-1}\mathrm{Gpc}$ 的模拟盒子，最多使用 $3000^3$ 个粒子。

Result: DarkEmulator2 achieves subpercent accuracy in reproducing the nonlinear matter power spectrum up to the particle Nyquist scale $k_{\mathrm{Ny}} \simeq 10\,h\,\mathrm{Mpc}^{-1}$ for $3000^3$-particle simulations. It maintains calibrated accuracy over its target wavenumber range, though high-$k$ performance depends on simulation resolution and shot noise. Validation on independent test suites confirms robustness, and cross-comparisons with public emulators (e.g., CosmicEmu, EuclidEmulator2) and fitting formulas (e.g., HALOFIT, HMCode) reveal systematic, parameter-dependent residuals, highlighting areas of inter-model tension.
\n\n
DarkEmulator2 在 $3000^3$ 粒子模拟中重现非线性物质功率谱的精度达到亚百分比水平，直至粒子奈奎斯特尺度 $k_{\mathrm{Ny}} \simeq 10\,h\,\mathrm{Mpc}^{-1}$。在其目标波数范围内保持标定精度，但高-$k$ 性能依赖于模拟分辨率和散粒噪声。在独立测试集上的验证证实了其鲁棒性；与公开模拟器（如 CosmicEmu、EuclidEmulator2）及拟合公式（如 HALOFIT、HMCode）的交叉比较揭示了系统性、依赖于参数的残差，凸显了模型间不一致的区域。

Conclusion: DarkEmulator2 demonstrates that incorporating physically motivated auxiliary inputs and multi-resolution simulation data into a unified neural network framework significantly improves the accuracy, generalization, and efficiency of nonlinear power spectrum emulation in extended cosmological models. It provides a reliable tool for precision cosmology with upcoming LSS surveys and sets a methodological benchmark for future emulator development in computational cosmology.
\n\n
DarkEmulator2 表明，在统一神经网络框架中引入物理动机明确的辅助输入和多分辨率模拟数据，可显著提升扩展宇宙学模型中非线性功率谱模拟器的精度、泛化能力和效率。它为即将开展的大尺度结构巡天提供了可靠的精密宇宙学工具，并为未来计算宇宙学中的模拟器开发设立了方法论基准。

Abstract: \textsc{DarkEmulator2} is a neural network emulator of the nonlinear matter power spectrum in a nine-dimensional $w_0 w_a νo \mathrm{CDM}$ parameter space, developed as the emulator component of the \textsc{Dark Quest II} (DQ2) program. It is trained on simulations generated with the \textsc{Ginkaku} code, whose numerical implementation, accuracy tests, and post-processing pipeline are described in the companion paper. The design follows a unified strategy: in addition to the cosmological parameter vector, we supplement the neural network's inputs with three families of physically motivated auxiliary quantities -- the linear matter power spectrum, descriptors of the simulation resolution, and a low-dimensional summary of the initial Gaussian random field -- that are expected to improve generalization across the parameter space. Training a single network jointly across three simulation resolution tiers allows the emulator to exploit a small number of high-resolution simulations while retaining broad coverage from lower-resolution simulations. For a $L_{\mathrm{box}}=1\,\hiGpc$ box with $N=3000^{3}$ particles, the emulator reproduces the simulated matter power spectrum to subpercent accuracy up to the particle Nyquist scale, $k_{\mathrm{Ny}}\simeq 10\,\hMpci$. The emulator remains accurate over the calibrated wavenumber range, while its highest-$k$ predictions depend on the simulation resolution and shot noise. We validate the emulator on independent test suites and, through a cross-comparison with several public emulators and widely used fitting formulas, characterize the inter-model consistency and the parameter-dependent trends in their residuals.

</details>


### [4] [Exploring non-Poisson satellite occupation in HOD models and its impact on 2- and 3-point galaxy clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28644)
*Antoine Rocher*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: This paper challenges the standard Poisson assumption for satellite galaxy counts in Halo Occupation Distribution (HOD) models by introducing the Conway-Maxwell-Poisson (CMP) distribution, which adds a single parameter $ν$ to model sub- and super-Poissonian variance. The authors develop analytical and numerical tools to implement CMP in HOD and show that non-Poisson statistics significantly affect small-scale clustering (up to 10%) and counts-in-cylinders (∼30%), but have minimal impact (<2%) on large-scale power spectrum and bispectrum measurements ($k_{\\mathrm{max}} < 0.3$).
\n\n
本文通过引入Conway-Maxwell-Poisson（CMP）分布，对Halo Occupation Distribution（HOD）模型中卫星星系数目服从Poisson分布的标准假设提出挑战。CMP分布仅增加一个参数$ν$，用于刻画次Poisson和超Poisson方差行为。作者开发了实现CMP的解析近似与数值方法，并表明非Poisson统计显著影响小尺度成团性（高达10%）和cylinder内计数（∼30%），但对大尺度功率谱和bispectrum测量（$k_{\\mathrm{max}} < 0.3$）的影响很小（<2%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Standard HOD models assume that the number of satellite galaxies in a dark matter halo of fixed mass follows a Poisson distribution, implying that the variance equals the mean. However, this may not reflect the true physics of galaxy formation, where feedback processes or assembly bias could induce sub- or super-Poissonian fluctuations. Testing this assumption is crucial for accurate modeling of galaxy clustering, especially as upcoming surveys probe increasingly smaller scales.
\n\n
标准HOD模型假设在固定质量的暗物质晕中，卫星星系的数量服从Poisson分布，即方差等于均值。然而，这可能无法反映星系形成的物理实际，其中反馈过程或assembly bias可能导致次Poisson或超Poisson涨落。检验这一假设对于精确建模星系成团性至关重要，尤其是随着新一代巡天探测越来越小的尺度。

Method: The authors replace the Poisson satellite occupation distribution with the Conway-Maxwell-Poisson (CMP) distribution, parametrized by $ν$, which controls the deviation from Poisson statistics ($ν = 1$ recovers Poisson). They derive an analytical approximation for the CMP mean parameter $λ$ and construct a numerical interpolation scheme between small- and large-$λ$ limits to ensure computational efficiency and accuracy (∼5% for $0.5 < ν < 2$). Mock galaxy catalogs are generated using the \\texttt{HODDIES} package, and clustering statistics—including projected correlation functions, multipoles of the anisotropic correlation function, counts-in-cylinders (CiC), and the tree-level galaxy bispectrum in the Sugiyama basis—are computed and compared across different $ν$ values.
\n\n
作者将Poisson卫星占据分布替换为Conway-Maxwell-Poisson（CMP）分布，该分布由参数$ν$控制对Poisson统计的偏离（$ν = 1$时恢复Poisson）。他们推导了CMP均值参数$λ$的解析近似，并构建了一个在小$λ$与大$λ$极限之间的数值插值方案，以保证计算效率和精度（在$0.5 < ν < 2$范围内精度约5%）。利用\\texttt{HODDIES}包生成模拟星系星表，并计算多种成团性统计量——包括投影相关函数、各向异性相关函数的monopole与quadrupole、cylinder内计数（CiC）以及Sugiyama基下的tree-level galaxy bispectrum——并在不同$ν$值下进行比较。

Result: Non-Poissonian satellite occupations significantly alter small-scale clustering: projected correlation functions deviate by up to $10\%$, and monopole/quadrupole moments by $\sim5\%$. Counts-in-cylinders (CiC) are highly sensitive, showing variations up to $\sim30\%$. In contrast, the tree-level galaxy bispectrum in the Sugiyama basis is only weakly affected, with deviations $<2\%$ for $k_{\\mathrm{max}} = 0.3 \, h/\mathrm{Mpc}$. The results demonstrate that while satellite occupation variance matters for small-scale statistics, its impact on large-scale cosmological inference from power spectrum and bispectrum is negligible within current observational limits.
\n\n
非Poisson卫星占据显著改变小尺度成团性：投影相关函数偏差高达$10\%$，monopole与quadrupole矩偏差约$5\%$；cylinder内计数（CiC）极为敏感，变化可达$\sim30\%$。相比之下，在Sugiyama基下的tree-level galaxy bispectrum受影响很弱，在$k_{\\mathrm{max}} = 0.3 \, h/\mathrm{Mpc}$时偏差小于$2\%$。结果表明，尽管卫星占据方差对小尺度统计量很重要，但在当前观测限制下，其对大尺度功率谱和bispectrum宇宙学推断的影响可忽略。

Conclusion: The Poisson assumption for satellite galaxy occupation in HOD models is insufficient for high-precision small-scale analyses, and the CMP distribution provides a flexible, minimal extension to account for non-Poissonian variance. However, for cosmological parameter estimation using large-scale structure data limited to $k_{\\mathrm{max}} < 0.3 \, h/\mathrm{Mpc}$, the impact of non-Poisson satellite statistics on power spectrum and bispectrum is subdominant and unlikely to bias results. Future analyses targeting small scales or higher-order statistics like CiC must incorporate more general occupation distributions.
\n\n
HOD模型中对卫星星系占据采用Poisson假设在高精度小尺度分析中是不足的，而CMP分布提供了一个灵活且极简的扩展以纳入非Poisson方差。然而，对于使用限制在$k_{\\mathrm{max}} < 0.3 \, h/\mathrm{Mpc}$的大尺度结构数据进行宇宙学参数估计而言，非Poisson卫星统计对功率谱和bispectrum的影响是次要的，不太会导致偏差。未来针对小尺度或CiC等高阶统计量的分析必须采用更一般的占据分布。

Abstract: Understanding the connection between galaxies and dark matter halos is a central challenge in modern cosmology. The Halo Occupation Distribution (HOD) framework provides a widely used statistical description of how galaxies populate dark matter halos, enabling precise modelling of galaxy clustering. A common assumption in standard HOD models is that the number of satellite galaxies follows a Poisson distribution at fixed halo mass. In this work, we revisit this assumption and introduce the Conway-Maxwell-Poisson (CMP) distribution as a minimal extension of of the Poisson model, which add a single parameter, $ν$, to explore sub- and super-Poisson behaviour. Abstract: We present a control-variate method for reducing the variance of power spectrum covariance matrix estimates from simulations of large-scale structure. The key idea is to pair each mock simulation with a cheap Zeldovich-approximation realization sharing the same initial conditions, and to use the known statistical properties of the Zeldovich field to remove correlated sample variance from the covariance estimator. Under a Gaussian disconnected approximation, we derive fully analytic expressions for both the optimal control-variate coefficient, $β(k,\ell;k',\ell')$, and the corresponding correlation, $ρ(k,\ell;k',\ell')$, in terms of the auto- and cross-power spectra of the target and control fields. In the monopole case, the correlation takes the particularly simple form $ρ(k,k') = r^2(k),r^2(k')$, where $r(k)$ is the standard cross-correlation coefficient between the target and Zeldovich fields, implying that covariance estimation remains highly efficient whenever the two fields are strongly correlated. For masked redshift-space lognormal mocks, resembling Luminous Red Galaxies from the Dark Energy Spectroscopic Instrument (DESI), we find that the control-variate estimator reduces the variance of the covariance matrix by approximately an order of magnitude on large scales, $k \lesssim 0.05\,h\,{\rm Mpc}^{-1}$, precisely where accurate covariance estimation is most challenging. The gains are smaller for higher $k$ but typically accelerate convergence by a factor of 2-3, substantially lowering the computational cost of covariance estimation for current and upcoming large-scale structure surveys. Due to its simplicity, this method is readily implementable in current imaging and spectroscopic surveys (e.g., DESI, Euclid, LSST, PFS, SPHEREx).

Abstract: Motivated by trans-Planckian issues in inflation, we determine the Hilbert space and amplitudes of quantum perturbations in the general low-energy effective theory of (multi-)field inflation without relying on the sub-horizon limit. The scalar sector is the most intricate, featuring field mixings and second-class constraints, which we handle using Dirac brackets. These results enable us to estimate the magnitude of higher-derivative corrections. In the specific case of slow-roll inflation, such estimate can be expressed in terms of the first slow-roll parameter $ε$ for a given cutoff $Λ$. 本文构建了AdS$_{d+1}$和BTZ黑洞上Breitenlohner–Freedman标量场的精确Robin体到边传播子，将标准与交替量子化之间的双迹（double-trace）重整化群（RG）流几何化为一族体边界条件。作者从边值问题内在地导出了核的紫外（UV）和红外（IR）链展开，识别出每一阶的分支分裂，将边界CFT可观测的局域数据与仅对体探针可见的有限体深度结构分离开来。在BTZ背景下，获得了闭形式的Robin核及对应的准正规模（quasinormal-mode）轨迹族，每条轨迹连接$g=0$处的交替量子化极点与$g\\to\\infty$处的标准量子化极点。文章定位了一个例外点（exceptional point, EP）轨迹，在该点两条轨迹合并为Jordan块，并证明其作为双迹流中的非厄米相边界：穿越该点会重构谱的全局极点配对拓扑。不同于通过动量或频率解析延拓得到的全息EP，这一相变存在于量子化插值过程中，可通过调节物理Robin耦合在有限实动量和温度下实现。

Method: The authors solve the Klein–Gordon equation for a Breitenlohner–Freedman scalar with Robin boundary conditions $\\partial_n \\phi = g \\phi$ on AdS$_{d+1}$ and the BTZ black hole. They derive the bulk-to-boundary propagator $K_f$ directly from the boundary-value problem without introducing auxiliary fields. UV and IR asymptotic expansions are obtained by analyzing the near-boundary and near-horizon behavior of solutions. On BTZ, they compute the exact Robin kernel using mode sums and identify quasinormal frequencies as poles of the propagator. Exceptional points are located by solving for parameter values where the quasinormal-mode spectrum develops degeneracies with non-diagonalizable (Jordan block) structure.

Abstract: We introduce `dualGNN', an autoregressive message-passing GNN for sampling fine, regular triangulations (FRTs) of convex polytopes. dualGNN operates on a generalization of the dual graph of a triangulation, with edges labeled by `signed circuits' -- combinatorial invariants from oriented matroid theory which we show are both necessary and sufficient for exposing regularity. Method: The authors employ the S-wall formalism—a codimension-one defect implementing electromagnetic duality—to analyze the structure of surface charges and edge modes. Abstract: We revisit electric and magnetic surface charges and edge modes in four-dimensional Maxwell theory and QED on a spacetime with a finite spatial boundary. Abstract: In this thesis we study embedding formalisms for $\mathcal{N}$-extended anti-de Sitter (AdS) superspaces in four and five dimensions. 在引力子场$h_{\\mu\\nu}$的四阶以内，左手Weyl费米子的重整化引力有效作用量不包含任何宇称奇的局域或非局域项。模去紫外有限、微分同胚不变、宇称偶的抵消项后，该有效作用量精确等于Dirac费米子情形的一半。因此，Weyl（迹）反常纯为宇称偶，其系数为标准Dirac费米子值的一半。

Abstract: We consider the field theory of a quantum massless left handed fermion coupled to a background graviton field, $h_{μν}$, on four-dimensional Minkowski spacetime.   <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Fermionic degrees of freedom are theoretically well-motivated in beyond-Standard-Model cosmology and could leave distinctive imprints in primordial non-Gaussianity (e.g., via the cosmological collider), but their loop-level contributions have been poorly understood due to technical challenges in evaluating fermionic loop integrals in de Sitter space. 费米自由度在超越标准模型的宇宙学中具有坚实的理论动机，可能在原初非高斯性（例如通过Cosmological Collider机制）中留下独特印记，但由于在de Sitter空间中计算费米圈图积分存在技术困难，其圈图贡献长期未被充分理解。本文旨在通过给出费米气泡图的精确结果来填补这一空白。

Method: The authors develop two complementary analytical techniques: (1) a spectral decomposition method based on an exact identity for the product of fermionic propagators, which rewrites the bubble loop as an infinite sum over tree-level exchange diagrams; and (2) a Mellin-Barnes representation, where the loop integral is evaluated by summing residues from distinct pole families. They also demonstrate that the fermionic bubble can be obtained from the scalar bubble via differential operators acting on coupling parameters. These methods are applied to Yukawa interactions between fermions and the inflaton.

Result: The exact analytic expression for the fermionic bubble contribution to the primordial bispectrum is derived for arbitrary couplings. Conclusion: Fermionic loop effects in cosmological correlators can be computed exactly using novel analytic techniques, revealing that certain well-motivated interactions (like inflaton-fermion Yukawa couplings) produce no observable non-Gaussian signal due to symmetries or redundancies in the effective description. Abstract: Fermionic degrees of freedom are essential ingredients in cosmological collider physics and are well motivated by many phenomenological models beyond the Standard Model, but their signals remain largely unexplored due to the difficulty of computing loop diagrams. The first method is similar in spirit to spectral decomposition and is built directly from an identity for the product of propagators, which turns the bubble signal into an infinite sum of tree-level exchange signals. Abstract: We study the modular symmetry in magnetized compactifications. The zero-modes with different Scherk-Schwarz phases transform each other. 作者考虑一个具有 $\\phi^4$ 自相互作用和显式洛伦兹破缺的有质量标量场，定义在1+1维的三边星形图上，外端点施加Dirichlet边界条件。他们微扰地计算Casimir能量：领头阶来自涨落模的零点能，一阶修正如来自单圈费曼图。发散通过动量截断正规化，并利用盒子减除法（即减去无限“盒子”中的真空能）予以消除。关键在于，抵消项被设为位置依赖的，以尊重边界条件，这遵循一个将边界效应整合进算符乘积展开或有效作用量的重整化方案。

无论是领头阶还是洛伦兹破缺参数存在与否，一阶辐射修正的Casimir能量均为负值。这一负号符合物理直觉——在此类受限几何中Casimir力通常表现为吸引力。采用位置依赖抵消项成功吸收了由边界引起的发散，而盒子减除法确保了有限且物理上有意义的结果。

Conclusion: The paper demonstrates that a consistent renormalization of Casimir energy in bounded, Lorentz-violating quantum field theories requires position-dependent counterterms that encode boundary information. Abstract: In this paper, we compute the leading-order and first-order radiative corrections to the Casimir energy of a massive Lorentz-violating scalar field governed by a $φ^4$ interaction on a network. These counterterms emerge from a systematic renormalization program that consistently incorporates the effects of boundary conditions into the renormalization procedure. To eliminate divergences arising from vacuum energy contributions, we employ the box subtraction scheme in conjunction with cutoff regularization. 非积分几何通过引入非不变积分测度，为积分几何提供了一个数学上自洽的扩展。所得的广义 Radon 逆变换通过项 $f_A$ 内置了自然的正则化机制，为克服断层图像重建中与奇点相关的挑战提供了有前景的新途径。

Abstract: In the present paper, we first describe the principal basis of non-integral geometry.   <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation stems from the need to understand how vector fields—despite being typically associated with breaking isotropy—can leave observable imprints on primordial non-Gaussianity even when the background remains exactly isotropic. 动机源于理解矢量场如何即使在背景严格保持各向同性的情况下，仍能在原初非高斯性中留下可观测印记。以往分析集中于弱矢量区域（$h \ll 1$），其中矢量-标量转移产生一个按 $h^2 N_K^3$ 增强的局域型双谱。然而，强耦合区域（$h \gg 1$）的动力学和双谱形状尚不清楚。阐明这一区域对于完整刻画矢量支持的暴胀模型（vector-supported inflation）的现象学至关重要。

Method: The authors organize the inflationary dynamics using the dimensionless parameter $h = \\frac{\\rho_A}{\\rho_\\phi}$, where $\\rho_A$ and $\\rho_\\phi$ are the kinetic energy densities of the vector and scalar fields, respectively. In the large-$h$ limit, they perform a systematic low-energy EFT analysis: the heavy entropic perturbation (mass $\\sim h H$) is integrated out, yielding an effective action for the adiabatic curvature mode $\\zeta$. This effective theory features a time-dependent imaginary sound speed $c_s^2 < 0$, leading to transient tachyonic growth of $\\zeta$ before horizon exit. The bispectrum is computed using in-in formalism, identifying contributions from both contact interactions and exchange diagrams, and projected onto standard templates (local, equilateral, flattened) to quantify shape dependence on $h$.

作者使用无量纲参数 $h = \\frac{\\rho_A}{\\rho_\\phi}$（其中 $\\rho_A$ 和 $\\rho_\\phi$ 分别为矢量场和标量场的动能密度）来组织暴胀动力学。在大 $h$ 极限下，他们进行了系统的低能有效场论（EFT）分析：将质量约为 $h H$ 的重熵扰动模式积分掉，得到绝热曲率扰动 $\\zeta$ 的有效作用量。该有效理论具有随时间变化的虚声速（$c_s^2 < 0$），导致 $\\zeta$ 在视界穿越前发生瞬态的快子增长（tachyonic growth）。双谱通过 in-in 形式计算，识别出接触相互作用和交换图的贡献，并投影到标准模板（局域型、等边型、扁平型）上，以量化其形状对 $h$ 的依赖关系。

Result: In the $h \gg 1$ regime, three distinct bispectrum contributions are identified: (i) a flattened-enhanced signal scaling as $h^3$, consistent with prior results; (ii) a new flattened-enhanced component scaling as $h^2$; and (iii) a significant local-type projection scaling linearly with $h$. The competition between these terms leads to a crossover: for intermediate $h$, the bispectrum is dominated by the local shape, while at sufficiently large $h$, the flattened shapes dominate. Crucially, this rich structure arises even when the background spacetime is exactly isotropic, demonstrating that vector fields can imprint unique non-Gaussian signatures without violating statistical isotropy.

在 $h \gg 1$ 区域，识别出三种不同的双谱贡献：(i) 与先前结果一致的按 $h^3$ 增强的扁平型信号；(ii) 一种新的按 $h^2$ 增强的扁平型成分；(iii) 一个显著的、按 $h$ 线性增强的局域型投影。这些项之间的竞争导致交叉行为：在中等 $h$ 时，双谱由局域型主导；而在足够大的 $h$ 时，扁平型占优。关键的是，即使背景时空严格各向同性，这种丰富的结构依然存在，表明矢量场可在不破坏统计各向同性的情况下留下独特的非高斯印记。

Conclusion: The study establishes that kinetic-coupled vector fields in inflation generate a characteristic and $h$-dependent bispectrum with multiple enhanced shapes, including both flattened and local types. Abstract: We revisit the resulting bispectrum of inflation with kinetic-coupled vector fields by organizing the dynamics in terms of $h$, which measures the vector kinetic contribution relative to that of the scalar field. We evaluate the bispectrum in the strong-vector regime and derive a low-energy effective field theory (EFT) for the large-$h$ regime. For $h\gg1$, the entropic perturbation becomes heavy and can be integrated out; the remaining curvature mode has an imaginary sound speed and undergoes transient growth before horizon crossing. ### [25] [Krylov complexity has it all](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28681)
*Wolfgang Mück*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: This paper proves that Krylov complexity fully encodes the dynamics of a quantum operator, showing its equivalence to other known characterizations like Lanczos coefficients, return amplitude, and spectral density. It provides an explicit recursive algorithm to extract Lanczos coefficients from the Taylor expansion of Krylov complexity at $t=0$, and explains why such a reconstruction is not possible for spread complexity without extra dynamical data.
\n\n
本文证明了Krylov complexity完全编码了一个量子算符的动力学信息，表明其与Lanczos系数、return amplitude和spectral density等已知表征等价。文中给出了一个显式的递归算法，可以从Krylov complexity在$t=0$处的Taylor展开中提取Lanczos系数，并解释了为何对于spread complexity，若无额外动力学输入，则无法构造类似的重构算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation stems from the need to identify minimal yet complete descriptors of quantum operator dynamics. While quantities like the Lanczos coefficients, return amplitude, and spectral density are known to fully characterize operator growth, it remains important to understand whether newer concepts—such as Krylov complexity—also possess this completeness property. Establishing such equivalence would validate Krylov complexity as a fundamental diagnostic in quantum many-body dynamics and quantum chaos.
\n\n
动机源于寻找既能最小化又能完整描述量子算符动力学的量。尽管Lanczos系数、return amplitude和spectral density已被证明能完全刻画算符增长，但有必要探究新近提出的概念（如Krylov complexity）是否也具备这种完备性。确立这种等价性将验证Krylov complexity作为量子多体动力学和量子混沌中基本诊断工具的地位。

Method: The authors construct an explicit recursive algorithm that maps the Taylor series coefficients of Krylov complexity $C_K(t)$ around $t = 0$ to the Lanczos coefficients $b_n$. This relies on the known relation between operator moments and the Lanczos algorithm in the Krylov basis. By leveraging the structure of the Heisenberg equation of motion and the tridiagonal form of the Liouvillian in the Krylov basis, they invert the moment–Lanczos map using only $C_K(t)$. They also contrast this with spread complexity, arguing that its definition lacks sufficient dynamical constraints to enable a similar inversion without additional input.
\n\n
作者构建了一个显式递归算法，将Krylov complexity $C_K(t)$ 在 $t = 0$ 处的Taylor级数系数映射到Lanczos系数 $b_n$。该方法依赖于算符矩与Krylov基下Lanczos算法之间的已知关系。通过利用Heisenberg运动方程的结构以及Liouvillian在Krylov基下的三对角形式，他们仅使用 $C_K(t)$ 实现了矩–Lanczos映射的逆运算。此外，他们将此与spread complexity进行对比，指出后者因定义中缺乏足够的动力学约束，若无额外输入则无法实现类似反演。

Result: The main result is the demonstration that Krylov complexity contains all information necessary to reconstruct the full set of Lanczos coefficients, thereby proving its equivalence to other complete descriptors of operator dynamics. The recursive algorithm explicitly computes $b_n$ from the derivatives of $C_K(t)$ at $t=0$. In contrast, spread complexity is shown to be insufficient by itself for such reconstruction unless supplemented with independent dynamical data.
\n\n
主要结果是证明了Krylov complexity包含重构全部Lanczos系数所需的全部信息，从而证明其与其他算符动力学的完备描述量等价。递归算法显式地从 $C_K(t)$ 在 $t=0$ 处的导数计算出 $b_n$。相比之下，spread complexity被证明自身不足以完成此类重构，除非辅以独立的动力学数据。

Conclusion: Krylov complexity is established as a complete and self-contained characterization of quantum operator dynamics, on par with traditional quantities like the spectral density or Lanczos coefficients. This “proof of principle” supports its use as a fundamental tool in studies of quantum chaos, thermalization, and operator growth, while also clarifying the limitations of related notions such as spread complexity.
\n\n
Krylov complexity被确立为一种完整且自洽的量子算符动力学表征，与spectral density或Lanczos系数等传统量处于同等地位。这一“原理性证明”支持其作为研究量子混沌、热化和算符增长的基本工具，同时也阐明了spread complexity等相关概念的局限性。

Abstract: This paper establishes that Krylov complexity contains the entire information about the dynamics of a quantum operator, extending the list of equivalent quantities that can serve this purpose, such as the Lanczos coefficients, the return amplitude, and the spectral density. To demonstrate this equivalence, an explicit recursive algorithm is constructed to calculate Lanczos coefficients from the Taylor expansion of the Krylov complexity around $t=0$. Furthermore, the paper discusses the distinction between Krylov and spread complexity, clarifying why a similar recursive algorithm cannot exist for the latter without additional dynamical input. These results provide a ``proof of principle'' for using Krylov complexity as a complete characterization of operator evolution in quantum systems.

### [26] [Complex BPS Black Holes in AdS$_3\times S^3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28737)

This discrepancy motivates the search for alternative complex saddle geometries that respect both supersymmetry and the structure of the index.

Method: The authors construct smooth complex gravitational solutions with $\mathrm{AdS}_3\times S^3$ boundary conditions in the STU model using two distinct approaches: (1) starting from four-dimensional two-center BPS black hole solutions with complexified dipole charges, and (2) reducing six-dimensional BPS black string solutions under the constraint imposed by the BPS relation among conserved charges. They then analyze the resulting geometries' smoothness and periodicity properties by examining the Killing spinor equations and the regularity of the metric and gauge fields.

作者在STU模型中通过两种不同方法构造具有$\mathrm{AdS}_3\times S^3$边界条件的光滑复引力解：(1) 从带有复偶极荷的四维双中心BPS黑洞解出发；(2) 在守恒荷满足BPS关系的约束下，约化六维BPS黑弦解。随后，他们通过分析Killing旋量方程以及度规和规范场的正则性，研究所得几何的光滑性与周期性性质。

Conclusion: The work demonstrates that the gravitational dual of the supersymmetric index is not given by naïve Euclidean black holes but by smooth complex saddles—specifically BTZ $\times S^3$ geometries—whose structure is tightly constrained by supersymmetry and thermodynamics. Abstract: The correct gravitational representation of the supersymmetric index is a smooth complex solution, rather than the naïve Euclidean continuation of the Lorentizan BPS black hole. We construct such saddles for black holes with \(\mathrm{AdS}_3\times S^3\) boundary conditions in the STU model. We arrive at the same finite-temperature BTZ \(\times S^3\) geometries in two independent ways: from two-center four-dimensional BPS solutions with complex dipole charges, and from six-dimensional black strings after imposing the BPS relation among conserved charges. We analyze the resulting smoothness and periodicity conditions and show that they are precisely those required for globally well-defined supersymmetry and for the thermodynamic potentials of the supersymmetric index.

本文探讨了在(2+1)维引力中，引力路径积分中的虫洞贡献如何源于对偶CFT在大$N$极限下的不规则关联行为。研究表明，单边边界graviton的可观测量代数存在非平凡中心，因此本质上是不完备的；通过引入由单值性（monodromy）数据构成的可观测量代数对其进行完备化，并将该数据解释为单边黑洞的有效描述。对单值性数据施加正定性限制会引发某些观测量的大$N$不规则行为；通过对这些观测量进行筛选（即对非局域对称性进行规范固定），单个CFT中的黑洞态被完全移除，而两个CFT的情形下则保留了描述虫洞的希尔伯特子空间，导致配分函数表现为对纠缠两个CFT单值性自由度的量子门的系综平均，其中两个CFT不规则观测量之间的关联以虫洞项形式贡献于配分函数。

作者分析了(2+1)维渐近AdS引力中边界graviton可观测量的代数结构，证明其具有非平凡中心，表明该代数不完备。基于渐近对称性原理，他们通过自举（bootstrap）构造了一个泊松括号结构，将单值性（monodromy）数据纳入以扩展该代数——这些数据被解释为单边黑洞的有效自由度。为描述洛伦兹型多边界虫洞，他们对单值性数据施加正定性条件。随后，通过限制到不规则大$N$观测量作用平凡的希尔伯特子空间来移除这些观测量，并证明这等价于对由单值性观测量生成的非局域对称性进行规范固定。该方法被分别应用于单个和两个CFT，以研究其对黑洞态和虫洞态的影响。

对于单个CFT，规范固定非局域对称性会从谱中移除所有黑洞态，且过滤后的配分函数表现出明显的系综平均特征。对于两个CFT，在仅规范固定非局域对称性的全局部分后，一个描述连通虫洞几何的希尔伯特子空间得以保留。所得过滤配分函数等于对纠缠两个CFT单值性自由度的量子门的系综平均。关键的是，两个CFT中不规则观测量之间的关联仍然存在，并以虫洞项的形式体现在配分函数中。

Conclusion: The work provides a concrete mechanism linking wormhole contributions in the gravitational path integral to correlations among erratic large-$N$ observables in dual CFTs. It demonstrates that boundary graviton algebras must be completed with monodromy data to capture black hole and wormhole physics, and that gauging associated nonlocal symmetries implements a physical filtering that removes erratic fluctuations while preserving topological correlations. This supports the view that ensemble averaging in holography arises not from fundamental randomness but from coarse-graining over unphysical or erratic quantum gravitational degrees of freedom.

本工作提供了一个具体机制，将引力路径积分中的虫洞贡献与对偶CFT中不规则大$N$观测量之间的关联联系起来。研究表明，为捕捉黑洞与虫洞物理，边界graviton代数必须通过单值性数据进行完备化，而对相关非局域对称性的规范固定则实现了一种物理筛选：在去除不规则涨落的同时保留拓扑关联。这支持了一种观点，即全息理论中的系综平均并非源于基本随机性，而是对非物理或不规则的量子引力自由度进行粗粒化的结果。

Abstract: The wormhole contribution to the gravitational path integral may be interpreted as smooth remnant of correlations among the erratic large-$N$ behaviors of dual CFTs. In this work, we investigate this idea in (2+1)-dimensional gravity. We show that one-sided boundary gravitons are intrinsically incomplete in the sense that the associated observable algebra has a nontrivial center regardless of choices of boundary conditions. Based on asymptotic symmetries, we bootstrap a general Poisson bracket to construct completions of the boundary gravitons. In the simplest completion, the commutant of the boundary graviton observable algebra is given by an observable algebra of monodromy data which we interpret as an effective description of one-sided black holes. We show that, to describe Lorentzian multi-boundary wormholes, only the monodromy data with a positivity restriction is needed. The positivity restriction results in emergent erratic large-$N$ behaviors for some observables. We filter out the erratic observables by restricting to a subspace on which they act trivially. The monodromy observables generate nonlocal symmetries lack of corresponding local currents. We show that gauging the nonlocal symmetries is equivalent to filtering out the erratic observables. For one CFT, gauging the nonlocal symmetries at the quantum level removes all black hole states. Filtering the partition function of CFTs leads to an apparent ensemble averaging. For two CFTs, a Hilbert subspace describing wormholes survives after gauging global part of the nonlocal symmetries. The filtered partition function of the two CFTs is an ensemble average over quantum gates entangling the monodromy degrees of freedom the two CFTs. The correlation between the erratic observables of the two CFTs is preserved, which contributes to the filtered partition function as a wormhole term.

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: This paper shows that two-dimensional gravity—specifically Jackiw-Teitelboim (JT) gravity—naturally arises from the holographic renormalization group (RG) flow of a 2d CFT with a 3d Einstein-AdS$_3$ bulk dual. The RG-corrected effective action at finite energy scale yields a 2d scalar-tensor theory, where the bulk radial lapse function becomes the dilaton. The standard T$\bar{\text{T}}$ deformation appears as a special case when the Fefferman-Graham gauge is imposed. The derivation is robust under holographic renormalization and extends to a family of boundary conditions, offering a first-principles origin for JT gravity at finite cutoff beyond Fefferman-Graham coordinates.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation stems from the ``GR from RG'' program, which seeks to derive gravitational dynamics as an emergent phenomenon from quantum field theory via the renormalization group. Specifically, the authors aim to understand how lower-dimensional gravity theories—like JT gravity—can emerge intrinsically from the RG flow of a boundary CFT without imposing specific asymptotic structures (e.g., Fefferman-Graham gauge). This addresses the question of whether finite-cutoff holography can yield dynamical gravity in the boundary theory from first principles.
\n\n
动机源于“GR from RG”纲领，该纲领试图通过重整化群将引力动力学视为量子场论中涌现的现象。作者特别关注如何从边界CFT的RG流中内在地导出低维引力理论（如JT引力），而不预设特定的渐近结构（例如Fefferman-Graham规范）。这旨在回答：有限截断全息是否能从第一性原理出发，在边界理论中产生动力学引力。

Method: The authors start with a generic 2d CFT that admits a 3d holographic dual described by pure Einstein gravity in AdS$_3$. They analyze the holographic RG flow by integrating out bulk degrees of freedom down to a finite radial cutoff surface. Using a general ADM-like decomposition of the bulk metric with a nontrivial radial lapse function $N(r,x)$, they derive the RG-modified boundary action. This action is shown to take the form of a 2d scalar-tensor gravity theory, where the lapse function becomes the dilaton. They compare this to the known T$\bar{\text{T}}$ deformation in the Fefferman-Graham limit ($N=1$) and verify consistency via holographic renormalization. Finally, they generalize the result to a one-parameter family of mixed boundary conditions.
\n\n
作者从一个具有三维AdS$_3$爱因斯坦引力对偶的二维CFT出发，通过将体空间自由度积分到有限径向截断面来分析全息RG流。他们采用ADM类分解，引入非平凡的径向lapse函数$N(r,x)$，并由此导出RG修正的边界作用量。该作用量被证明具有二维标量-张量引力的形式，其中lapse函数成为dilaton场。他们在Fefferman-Graham极限（$N=1$）下将其与已知的T$\bar{\text{T}}$形变对比，并通过全息重整化验证其自洽性。最后，他们将结果推广至一族混合边界条件。

Result: The main result is that the holographic RG flow of a 2d CFT induces an effective 2d gravity theory on the cutoff surface, specifically Jackiw-Teitelboim gravity in its simplest realization. The dynamical dilaton $\Phi$ is identified with the bulk radial lapse: $\Phi \sim N(r_c, x)$, where $r_c$ is the cutoff radius. The standard T$\bar{\text{T}}$-deformed CFT action is recovered when $N=1$, corresponding to the Fefferman-Graham gauge. The framework remains consistent under holographic renormalization and extends to a continuous family of boundary conditions parameterized by a single parameter, demonstrating universality of the induced gravity mechanism.
\n\n
主要结果是：二维CFT的全息RG流在截断面上诱导出一个有效的二维引力理论，在最简情形下即为Jackiw-Teitelboim引力。动力学dilaton场$\Phi$被识别为体空间径向lapse函数：$\Phi \sim N(r_c, x)$，其中$r_c$为截断半径。当$N=1$（对应Fefferman-Graham规范）时，标准的T$\bar{\text{T}}$形变CFT作用量被恢复。该框架在全息重整化下保持自洽，并可推广至由单参数刻画的一族边界条件，表明诱导引力机制具有普适性。

Conclusion: The paper provides a first-principles derivation of JT gravity as an emergent theory from the holographic RG flow of a 2d CFT, without assuming Fefferman-Graham asymptotics. This supports the broader ``GR from RG'' paradigm by showing that gravitational dynamics—including a dynamical dilaton and diffeomorphism-invariant action—arise naturally from RG coarse-graining in the boundary theory. The result bridges finite-cutoff holography, T$\bar{\text{T}}$ deformations, and lower-dimensional quantum gravity, offering a unified perspective on how spacetime and gravity emerge from quantum information flow.

本文在不预设Fefferman-Graham渐近结构的前提下，从二维CFT的全息RG流出发，为JT引力提供了第一性原理的推导。这一结果支持了更广泛的“GR from RG”范式，表明引力动力学——包括动力学dilaton和微分同胚不变的作用量——自然地源于边界理论中的RG粗粒化过程。该工作连接了有限截断全息、T$\bar{\text{T}}$形变与低维量子引力，为时空和引力如何从量子信息流中涌现提供了统一视角。

Abstract: We demonstrate how the two-dimensional gravity emerges within ``GR from RG'' program initiated in \cite{Adami:2025pqr, Sheikh-Jabbari:2026uol}. To achieve this, we consider a generic 2d CFT with a 3d holographic description, which we assume to be well-described by pure Einstein-AdS$_3$ gravity in the bulk. We study the holographic RG flow for the 2d CFT action and show that the renormalization group (RG) corrected action at an arbitrary energy scale contains a 2d scalar-tensor gravity theory. In the simplest case, the flow induces Jackiw-Teitelboim (JT) gravity, where the bulk radial lapse function seeds the dynamical dilaton field of the JT gravity. We show that the standard T$\bar{\text{T}}$ deformation of the 2d CFT is recovered as a special case in the Fefferman-Graham limit where the lapse is fixed. We further establish the robustness of the RG induced gravity picture by verifying its consistency under holographic renormalization and by generalizing the result to a one-parameter family of boundary conditions. Our results provide a first-principles derivation of the JT gravity at a finite cutoff as an intrinsic manifestation of the holographic RG flow in a non-Fefferman-Graham gauge

</details>

### [29] [Influence of the QCD Analogue of the Inverse Compton Effect on the Transverse Momentum and Pseudorapidity Distributions of Secondary Particles in pp Collisions at sqrt (s)= 30 GeV, 510 GeV, and 14 TeV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27468)
*M. Alizada,M. Suleymanov*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: This paper studies the QCD analogue of the inverse Compton effect (ICE) in quark--gluon scattering within proton--proton collisions using PYTHIA~8.316 simulations, showing that ICE contributions grow with collision energy and dominate over direct Compton-like (DCE) processes at high energies, especially in central pseudorapidity regions.
\n\n
本文在质子-质子碰撞中利用PYTHIA~8.316模拟研究了夸克-胶子散射过程中QCD类逆康普顿效应（ICE）的影响，表明随着碰撞能量升高，ICE贡献逐渐增强并在高能下超过直接康普顿类（DCE）过程，尤其在中心赝快度区域占主导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation is to understand how partonic kinematic configurations—specifically those where the incoming quark carries more energy than the gluon (ICE)—affect observable particle distributions in high-energy proton--proton collisions, and how their relative importance evolves with collision energy compared to the complementary DCE regime.
\n\n
动机在于理解部分子运动学构型——特别是入射夸克携带比胶子更高能量的构型（ICE）——如何影响高能质子-质子碰撞中的可观测粒子分布，并探究其相对于互补的DCE机制的重要性如何随碰撞能量演化。

Method: The authors employ Monte Carlo simulations using the PYTHIA~8.316 event generator to simulate $pp$ collisions at $\sqrt{s} = 30$ GeV, 510 GeV, and 14 TeV. They isolate ICE and DCE contributions by classifying parton-level events based on the energy fractions ($x_1$, $x_2$) of the incoming quark and gluon, then analyze resulting $p_T$ and $\eta$ distributions of secondary particles.

作者使用PYTHIA~8.316事件生成器进行蒙特卡洛模拟，模拟$\sqrt{s} = 30$ GeV、510 GeV和14 TeV下的$pp$碰撞。通过根据入射夸克和胶子的能量分数（$x_1$, $x_2$）对部分子级事件分类，分离出ICE和DCE贡献，并分析次级粒子的$p_T$和$\eta$分布。

Result: As $\sqrt{s}$ increases from 30 GeV to 14 TeV, the ICE contribution becomes comparable to or exceeds DCE over a wide $p_T$ range. The ICE/DCE ratio deviates from unity mainly in the central pseudorapidity region ($|\eta| \simeq 0$), associated with symmetric parton momentum fractions ($x_1 \sim x_2$), while approaching unity in peripheral regions. This growth of ICE is attributed to enhanced small-$x$ gluon interactions at higher energies.
\n\n
随着$\sqrt{s}$从30 GeV增至14 TeV，ICE贡献在宽广的$p_T$范围内变得与DCE相当甚至更大。ICE/DCE比值主要在中心赝快度区域（$|\eta| \simeq 0$）偏离1，对应于对称的部分子动量分数（$x_1 \sim x_2$），而在边缘区域趋近于1。这种ICE增长归因于高能下小-$x$胶子相互作用的增强。

Conclusion: The study demonstrates that ICE-like kinematic configurations become increasingly significant in high-energy $pp$ collisions due to the rising dominance of small-$x$ gluons, altering the balance between ICE and DCE processes and impacting transverse momentum and pseudorapidity distributions, particularly in central rapidity regions.

研究表明，由于小-$x$胶子在高能$pp$碰撞中日益主导，ICE类运动学构型变得愈发重要，改变了ICE与DCE过程之间的平衡，并显著影响横动量和赝快度分布，尤其是在中心快度区域。

Abstract: Within the framework of numerical simulations, this work investigates the influence of the QCD analogue of the inverse Compton effect (ICE) in the quark--gluon scattering process qg \rightarrow qg on the transverse momentum p_T and pseudorapidity eta distributions of secondary particles produced in proton--proton collisions at energies sqrt{s}=30 GeV, 510 GeV, 14 TeV. In the present context, ICE refers to a class of parton-level kinematic configurations in which the incoming quark carries a larger fraction of energy than the gluon, in contrast to the complementary DCE regime. The simulations were performed using the PYTHIA~8.316 event generator. It is shown that the relative contributions of ICE and DCE strongly depend on the collision energy. As the energy increases from sqrt{s}=30 GeV to sqrt{s}=14 TeV, the ICE contribution becomes comparable to or exceeds the DCE contribution over a broad p_T range. The analysis of pseudorapidity distributions demonstrates that deviations of the ICE/DCE ratio from unity appear predominantly in the central region |eta| simeq 0, corresponding to symmetric partonic configurations x_1 sim x_2, whereas in peripheral regions the ratio approaches unity. The obtained results indicate that, with increasing collision energy, the contribution of ICE-like processes grows due to the enhanced role of gluon collisions in the small-x region.

</details>


### [30] [Neutrino-antineutrino superfluidity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27506)
*Damiano F. G. Fiorillo,Georg G. Raffelt,Günter Sigl*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: This paper investigates whether collective neutrino interactions can induce superfluid-like pairing instabilities in dense astrophysical environments. It concludes that such instabilities require discontinuities in the neutrino occupation number (e.g., a sharp Fermi surface), and that discretized energy spectra in numerical treatments may artificially produce these instabilities, which vanish in the continuum limit.
\n\n
本文研究了在致密天体物理环境中，集体中微子相互作用是否能诱导类似超流体的配对不稳定性。结论是，此类不稳定性需要中微子占据数存在不连续性（例如尖锐的费米面），而数值计算中离散化的能谱可能人为产生这些不稳定性，但在连续极限下会消失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation stems from the analogy between dense neutrino gases and fermionic systems exhibiting superfluidity or superconductivity. The authors aim to determine whether standard weak interactions among neutrinos—despite being feeble—can lead to pairing instabilities akin to Cooper pairing, particularly in environments like core-collapse supernovae or neutron star mergers where neutrino densities are extremely high.
\n\n
动机源于致密中微子气体与表现出超流性或超导性的费米系统之间的类比。作者旨在确定标准弱相互作用下的中微子——尽管相互作用微弱——是否能在中微子密度极高的环境中（如核心坍缩超新星或中子星并合）引发类似于库珀配对（Cooper pairing）的配对不稳定性。

Method: The authors analyze the linear response of a homogeneous neutrino gas under standard weak interactions, focusing on the emergence of pairing instabilities via the Bogoliubov–de Gennes formalism or analogous many-body frameworks. They examine the role of the occupation number $f(E)$ in momentum space, particularly whether smooth distributions suppress instabilities while discontinuities (like a step-function Fermi surface) permit them. They also investigate how discretization of energy levels in numerical simulations introduces artificial features resembling such discontinuities.
\n\n
作者在标准弱相互作用下分析均匀中微子气体的线性响应，聚焦于通过Bogoliubov–de Gennes形式或类似的多体框架来研究配对不稳定性是否出现。他们考察动量空间中占据数 $f(E)$ 的作用，特别是光滑分布是否会抑制不稳定性，而不连续性（如阶跃函数型费米面）是否允许不稳定性存在。此外，他们还研究了数值模拟中能级离散化如何引入类似不连续性的人工特征。

Result: The analysis shows that pairing instabilities in neutrino gases arise only if the occupation number exhibits a discontinuity—such as at a sharp Fermi surface—mirroring the mechanism behind BCS superconductivity. In realistic astrophysical scenarios with smooth thermal distributions, such instabilities are absent. However, when energy spectra are discretized (as in lattice-based or mode-truncated simulations), artificial discontinuities can emerge, leading to spurious superfluid instabilities that vanish as the continuum limit is restored.
\n\n
分析表明，中微子气体中的配对不稳定性仅在占据数存在不连续性时才会出现——例如在尖锐费米面处——这与BCS超导机制类似。在具有平滑热分布的实际天体物理场景中，此类不稳定性并不存在。然而，当能谱被离散化时（如基于格点或截断模态的模拟），会出现人为的不连续性，导致虚假的超流不稳定性，而这些不稳定性在恢复连续极限后消失。

Conclusion: Standard weak interactions alone do not induce genuine neutrino superfluidity in astrophysical environments with smooth momentum distributions. Caution is warranted when interpreting numerical results from discretized models, as they may exhibit unphysical pairing instabilities. While effective field theories such as WET and SMEFT are formulated in terms of Wilson coefficients of the relevant operators, with correlations characteristic of the SM and of specific NP scenarios, the most direct tests of the SM and its extensions are, in my opinion, correlations among different observables, like branching ratios of numerous decays, that are characteristic of particular new animalcula at work

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: There has been speculation that large corrections to the inflationary tensor power spectrum arise from either second-order classical scalar-induced effects or 1-loop quantum corrections. However, these two contributions are not independent, leading to ambiguity in their interpretation and potential double-counting. Clarifying their relationship—and how divergences in each piece cancel in the full quantum result—is essential for reliable predictions of primordial gravitational waves and for validating numerical approaches like N-body simulations or EFTofLSS-inspired methods applied to early-universe cosmology.

人们推测暴胀张量功率谱的大修正可能源于二阶经典标量诱导效应或一阶圈图量子修正。然而，这两类贡献并非独立，导致解释上的模糊性甚至重复计数。澄清二者关系——尤其是它们各自的发散如何在完整量子结果中抵消——对于可靠预言原初引力波、验证诸如N-body模拟或受EFTofLSS启发的早期宇宙数值方法至关重要。

Method: The authors compute the full 1-loop correction to the tensor power spectrum sourced by a massless minimally coupled scalar field during inflation. They decompose this quantum correction into a classical (scalar-induced) part and a vacuum (quantum fluctuation) part using dimensional regularization. They analyze the infrared (IR) behavior of each component, identifying divergences in the classical term. To enable numerical evaluation, they introduce a hard momentum cutoff and demonstrate that the IR sensitivity translates into a cubic divergence ($\propto \Lambda_{\text{IR}}^3$). Finally, they propose a "non-perturbative renormalization" procedure to subtract unphysical divergent pieces while preserving observable physics.
\n\n
作者计算了暴胀期间由无质量极小耦合标量场引起的一阶圈图对张量功率谱的完整修正。他们利用维度正规化将该量子修正分解为经典（标量诱导）部分和真空（量子涨落）部分，分析各部分的红外（IR）行为，并识别出经典项中的发散。为进行数值评估，他们引入硬动量截断，证明红外敏感性表现为立方发散（$\propto \Lambda_{\text{IR}}^3$）。最后，他们提出一种“非微扰重整化”程序，在保留可观测物理的同时减去非物理的发散项。

Result: In dimensional regularization, the classical (scalar-induced) contribution to the 1-loop tensor power spectrum is infrared (IR) divergent, while the full 1-loop quantum result is finite due to exact cancellation between classical and vacuum parts. Conclusion: The apparent large corrections to the inflationary tensor power spectrum from scalar-induced effects are entangled with quantum vacuum contributions. Abstract: Large corrections to the inflationary tensor power spectrum have been speculated to emerge either as second-order scalar-induced classical effects, or as 1-loop quantum corrections. Choosing the example of a massless minimally coupled scalar field, we show how the full 1-loop result can be divided into its classical and vacuum parts. If this can be implemented in realistic systems, it could consolidate numerical studies of inflationary scalar-induced gravitational waves.

Abstract: We study the sensitivity of current and future collider observables to top-quark SMEFT operators through a one-operator-at-a-time analysis. This approach clarifies the role of specific observables in the top-quark SMEFT program and highlights the significant improvement in sensitivity expected at future facilities.

Clear resonance peaks are found for $P_{c\bar{c}}(4312)$ ($J^P=1/2^-$) and $P_{c\bar{c}}(4457)$ ($J^P=3/2^-$), while $P_{c\bar{c}}(4380)$ with $J^P=5/2^-$ is suppressed due to the lack of tensor interactions from $Λ_c$ exchange. The total cross section reaches the microbarn level at resonance peaks.
\n\n
$u$-道 $Λ_c$ 交换通过 $\bar{D}^{(*)}Σ_c$ 中间态显著增强了再散射效应，使得 $\bar{D}^{(*)}Σ_c^{(*)}$ 与 $\bar{D}^{(*)}Λ_c$ 通道的贡献相当。研究发现 $P_{c\bar{c}}(4312)$（$J^P=1/2^-$）和 $P_{c\bar{c}}(4457)$（$J^P=3/2^-$）呈现清晰的共振峰，而具有 $J^P=5/2^-$ 的 $P_{c\bar{c}}(4380)$ 因 $Λ_c$ 交换缺乏张量相互作用而被强烈压低。总截面在共振峰处达到微靶（microbarn）量级。

Conclusion: Hidden-charm pentaquark states can be dynamically generated in $πN \to J/ψN$ scattering through hadronic rescattering mechanisms involving $u$-channel $Λ_c$ exchange, which is absent in photoproduction. This explains the non-observation of these states in $γp$ experiments and predicts observable signals in pion-induced reactions, offering a viable pathway to confirm their molecular nature.
\n\n
隐粲五夸克态可通过 $πN \to J/ψN$ 散射中的强子再散射机制动态产生，该机制依赖于光致产生中所缺失的 $u$-道 $Λ_c$ 交换。这解释了为何这些态在 $γp$ 实验中未被观测到，并预言它们在π介子诱导反应中应有可观测信号，为验证其分子态本质提供了可行途径。

Abstract: We investigate the dynamical generation of hidden-charm pentaquark resonances in the $πN \to J/ψN$ reaction utilizing an off-shell coupled-channel formalism. Motivated by the absence of pentaquark signals in $J/ψ$ photoproduction, we evaluate rescattering effects with two-body kernel amplitudes constructed from effective Lagrangians that explicitly incorporate $t$-channel meson and $u$-channel baryon exchanges. We demonstrate that the $u$-channel $Λ_c$ exchange, of which an analogous contribution is absent in the photoproduction kernel, greatly enhances the rescattering contributions through the $\bar{D}^{(*)}Σ_c$ intermediate states. Consequently, the $\bar{D}^{(*)}Σ_c^{(*)}$ channels yield contributions of comparable magnitude to the $\bar{D}^{(*)}Λ_c$ channels, directly leading to prominent pentaquark signals. The partial-wave analysis reveals that the $P_{c\bar{c}}(4312)$ and $P_{c\bar{c}}(4457)$ states emerge as clear peak structures with $J^P=1/2^-$ and $3/2^-$, respectively. In contrast, the $P_{c\bar{c}}(4380)$ and $J^P=5/2^-$ states are strongly suppressed because the $Λ_c$ exchange does not provide the required tensor interactions. The result for the total cross section reaches the microbarn level at the peak positions.

</details>

TL;DR: This paper extends the topological diagram approach (TDA) to study charmed baryon weak decays $\mathcal{B}_c \to \mathcal{B} V$, showing that only five independent TDA parameter sets are needed due to the Korner-Pati-Woo theorem. It derives symmetry relations from isospin, U-spin, and V-spin, extracts form factors via global fits, highlights the significance of tensor couplings ($A_2$, $B_2$), predicts branching fractions and other observables, and establishes a systematic framework testable by future experiments.

本文将拓扑图方法（Topological Diagram Approach, TDA）进一步应用于含矢量介子末态的粲重子弱衰变 $\mathcal{B}_c \to \mathcal{B} V$ 研究，利用 Korner-Pati-Woo 定理证明仅需五组独立的 TDA 参数。文中在 TDA 框架下显式推导了由同位旋（isospin）、U-自旋（U-spin）和 V-自旋（V-spin）对称性导致的不同衰变道之间的关系，并通过全局拟合实验数据提取了各拓扑图对应的分波贡献与形状因子。研究发现，由矢量介子与八重态重子张量相互作用诱导的形状因子 $A_2$ 和 $B_2$ 的大小与 $A_1$、$B_1$ 相当，表明张量耦合在 $\mathcal{B}_c \to \mathcal{B} V$ 衰变中具有重要意义。文章预测了所有 $\mathcal{B}_c \to \mathcal{B} V$ 通道的分支比及其他物理观测量（如上下不对称性、纵向极化及后续衰变可观测量），并与现有数据吻合良好，为理解此类衰变提供了系统性框架，可由未来实验进一步检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation stems from the need to systematically understand charmed baryon weak decays involving vector mesons in the final state, which are more complex than pseudoscalar-only decays due to additional helicity amplitudes and form factors. Existing theoretical treatments lack a unified framework incorporating flavor symmetries and topological dynamics; thus, the authors aim to refine the TDA by integrating the Korner-Pati-Woo theorem and SU(3) flavor symmetries to reduce model dependence and extract robust dynamical information.
\n\n
该研究的动机源于系统理解末态包含矢量介子的粲重子弱衰变的需求。这类衰变比仅含赝标量介子的衰变更复杂，因其涉及额外的螺旋度振幅和形状因子。现有理论处理缺乏一个统一框架来同时纳入味对称性与拓扑动力学；因此，作者旨在通过整合 Korner-Pati-Woo 定理和 SU(3) 味对称性来改进拓扑图方法（TDA），以减少模型依赖性并提取可靠的强子动力学信息。

Method: The authors employ the topological diagram approach (TDA) combined with the Korner-Pati-Woo theorem to reduce the number of independent amplitudes in $\mathcal{B}_c \to \mathcal{B} V$ decays. They impose isospin, U-spin, and V-spin symmetries to derive linear relations among decay amplitudes. Global fits to experimental branching fraction data are performed to extract partial wave contributions and form factors ($A_1, A_2, B_1, B_2$) associated with different topological diagrams (e.g., tree, color-suppressed, W-exchange). Predictions for branching ratios, up-down asymmetries, longitudinal polarizations, and secondary decay observables are then computed using the fitted parameters.
\n\n
作者采用拓扑图方法（TDA）并结合 Korner-Pati-Woo 定理，以减少 $\mathcal{B}_c \to \mathcal{B} V$ 衰变中独立振幅的数量。他们施加同位旋、U-自旋和 V-自旋对称性，推导出不同衰变振幅之间的线性关系。通过对实验分支比数据进行全局拟合，提取与不同拓扑图（如树图、色压低图、W-交换图）相关的分波贡献及形状因子（$A_1, A_2, B_1, B_2$）。随后利用拟合参数计算分支比、上下不对称性、纵向极化率以及次级衰变中的可观测量。

Result: The analysis shows that only five independent sets of TDA parameters are sufficient to describe all $\mathcal{B}_c \to \mathcal{B} V$ decays. The extracted form factors reveal that tensor-induced terms $A_2$ and $B_2$ are comparable in magnitude to the standard vector/axial-vector terms $A_1$ and $B_1$, indicating non-negligible tensor coupling contributions. Predicted branching fractions for most channels agree well with current experimental measurements. Additional observables—such as up-down asymmetries and longitudinal polarization fractions—are provided as testable predictions for future experiments.
\n\n
分析表明，仅需五组独立的 TDA 参数即可描述所有 $\mathcal{B}_c \to \mathcal{B} V$ 衰变。提取的形状因子显示，由张量相互作用诱导的项 $A_2$ 和 $B_2$ 在大小上与标准的矢量/轴矢量项 $A_1$、$B_1$ 相当，表明张量耦合贡献不可忽略。大多数通道的预测分支比与当前实验测量值吻合良好。文章还提供了其他可观测量（如上下不对称性和纵向极化分数）作为未来实验可检验的预测。

Conclusion: The study establishes a systematic and symmetry-constrained TDA framework for $\mathcal{B}_c \to \mathcal{B} V$ weak decays, demonstrating the importance of tensor interactions and providing precise predictions for a wide range of observables. This framework not only explains existing data but also offers clear targets for future high-statistics experiments at facilities like LHCb and Belle II to probe QCD dynamics in heavy baryon decays.
\n\n
本研究建立了一个受对称性约束的系统性 TDA 框架用于描述 $\mathcal{B}_c \to \mathcal{B} V$ 弱衰变，揭示了张量相互作用的重要性，并对多种可观测量给出了精确预测。该框架不仅能解释现有数据，还为 LHCb 和 Belle II 等高统计量实验提供了明确目标，以深入探索重子衰变中的 QCD 动力学。

Abstract: In this work, we further develop the application of the topological diagram approach (TDA) to charmed baryon weak decays $\mathcal{B}_c \to \mathcal{B} V$ with a vector meson in the final state. By incorporating the Korner-Pati-Woo theorem, we show that only five independent sets of TDA parameters are required. Relations among different decay channels arising from isospin, U-spin, and V-spin symmetries are explicitly derived within the TDA framework. Partial wave contributions and form factors associated with different topological diagrams are extracted from global fits to the experimental data. It is found that the form factors $A_2$ and $B_2$ induced from the tensor interaction of the vector mreson with octet baryons are comparable in magnitude to $A_1$ and $B_1$, implying the importance of the tensor coupling in $\mathcal{B}_c \to \mathcal{B} V$ decays. Branching fractions for all $\mathcal{B}_c\to \mathcal{B} V$ channels are predicted, and most measured modes are found to be in good agreement with current data. More physical observables, including up-down asymmetries, longitudinal polarizations as well as observables in the subsequent decays are also predicted. Our results provide a systematic framework for understanding charmed baryon weak decays with vector mesons and can be further tested with future experiments.

</details>


### [45] [Double D meson production in ultraperipheral $pp$, $pPb$ and $PbPb$ collisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28568)
*Ya-Ping Xie,Victor P. Goncalves*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: The paper studies $D^+ D^-$ pair production via photon–photon fusion in ultraperipheral $pp$, $pPb$, and $PbPb$ collisions at the LHC using the Brodsky–Lepage formalism under the heavy-quark approximation, predicting differential cross-sections including rapidity, transverse momentum, total transverse momentum, and momentum imbalance distributions, and concludes that such measurements are feasible during the High-Luminosity LHC run.
\n\n
本文利用Brodsky–Lepage形式在重夸克近似下研究了LHC上超外围$pp$、$pPb$和$PbPb$碰撞中通过光子–光子融合产生$D^+ D^-$对的过程，预测了包括快度、横动量、总横动量和动量不平衡分布在内的微分截面，并得出结论：在高亮度LHC运行期间，此类测量原则上是可行的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation stems from the opportunity to probe QCD dynamics and heavy-quarkonium-like processes in a clean environment offered by ultraperipheral heavy-ion and proton collisions at the LHC, where photon–photon interactions dominate. The $γγ \rightarrow D^+ D^-$ process provides a testbed for perturbative QCD factorization and the Brodsky–Lepage formalism in charm production, which is less explored compared to bottom or light quarks.
\n\n
动机源于LHC上超外围重离子和质子碰撞所提供的干净环境，其中光子–光子相互作用占主导地位，可用于探测QCD动力学和类重夸克偶素过程。$γγ \rightarrow D^+ D^-$过程为微扰QCD因子化和Brodsky–Lepage形式在粲夸克产生中的应用提供了检验平台，而相较于底夸克或轻夸克，粲夸克相关过程的研究仍较少。

Method: The authors employ the Brodsky–Lepage formalism within the heavy-quark approximation to compute the scattering amplitude for $γγ \rightarrow D^+ D^-$. They convolute this amplitude with equivalent photon fluxes from colliding protons or nuclei (using the Weizsäcker–Williams method) to obtain differential cross-sections for ultraperipheral $pp$, $pPb$, and $PbPb$ collisions. Predictions include standard observables like rapidity ($y$) and transverse momentum ($p_T$), as well as novel distributions: total transverse momentum ($|\vec{p}_{T,1} + \vec{p}_{T,2}|$) and momentum imbalance ($|\vec{p}_{T,1} - \vec{p}_{T,2}|$).
\n\n
作者在重夸克近似下采用Brodsky–Lepage形式计算$γγ \rightarrow D^+ D^-$的散射振幅，并将其与通过Weizsäcker–Williams方法得到的质子或原子核等效光子通量卷积，以获得超外围$pp$、$pPb$和$PbPb$碰撞的微分截面。预测包括标准观测量如快度（$y$）和横动量（$p_T$），以及新颖的分布：总横动量（$|\vec{p}_{T,1} + \vec{p}_{T,2}|$）和动量不平衡（$|\vec{p}_{T,1} - \vec{p}_{T,2}|$）。

Result: The computed cross-sections show measurable rates for $D^+ D^-$ photoproduction in all three collision systems ($pp$, $pPb$, $PbPb$) at LHC energies. The differential distributions—particularly the total transverse momentum and momentum imbalance—exhibit distinct kinematic features sensitive to the underlying production mechanism. The integrated cross-sections suggest that sufficient event yields could be accumulated during the High-Luminosity LHC phase to enable experimental observation.

计算得到的截面表明，在LHC能量下，三种碰撞体系（$pp$、$pPb$、$PbPb$）中$D^+ D^-$光致产生的事例率是可测的。微分分布——尤其是总横动量和动量不平衡——展现出对底层产生机制敏感的独特运动学特征。积分截面表明，在高亮度LHC阶段可积累足够事例以实现实验观测。

Conclusion: The study demonstrates that $γγ \rightarrow D^+ D^-$ in ultraperipheral collisions is theoretically tractable within the Brodsky–Lepage framework and experimentally accessible at the HL-LHC. The proposed new observables (total $p_T$ and momentum imbalance) offer additional handles to test QCD factorization and charm-quark dynamics in exclusive photoproduction.
\n\n
研究表明，在Brodsky–Lepage框架下，超外围碰撞中的$γγ \rightarrow D^+ D^-$过程在理论上是可处理的，并且在高亮度LHC上具有实验可行性。所提出的新型观测量（总$p_T$和动量不平衡）为检验QCD因子化和粲夸克在排他性光致产生中的动力学提供了额外手段。

Abstract: The production of a $D^+ D^-$ pair by photon - photon interactions at the Large Hadron Collider (LHC) is investigated considering ultraperipheral proton - proton ($pp$), proton - lead ($pPb$) and lead - lead ($PbPb$) collisions. Assuming that the scattering amplitude for the $γγ\rightarrow D^+ D^-$ process can be described by the Brodsky - Lepage formalism in the heavy - quark approximation, we derive the associated differential distributions and total cross - sections. In particular, in addition to the rapidity and transverse momentum distributions, usually presented in the literature, we also present predictions for the total transverse momentum and momentum imbalance distributions. Our results indicate that a future experimental analysis of this final state is, in principle, feasible during the high luminosity run of LHC.

</details>

*Pulak Banerjee,Chinmoy Dey,M. C. Kumar,Vaibhav Pandey,V. Ravindran*

\n\n
我们计算了 $H \rightarrow b + \bar{b} + g$ 振幅的无质量QCD两圈修正，包含维数正规化参数 $ε$ 的高阶项，这些结果是强子对撞机上底夸克湮灭产生希格斯加喷注过程的三圈虚修正所必需的输入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Higher-order perturbative corrections in QCD are essential for precision predictions of Higgs boson production and decay processes at the LHC. The specific process $H \rightarrow b\bar{b}g$ contributes to Higgs-plus-jet production through bottom-quark annihilation, and its two-loop amplitudes with $\\mathcal{O}(ε^2)$ or higher terms are needed to consistently construct infrared-subtracted three-loop virtual amplitudes.
\n\n
在LHC上对希格斯玻色子产生和衰变过程进行精确预言需要高阶QCD微扰修正。特定过程 $H \rightarrow b\bar{b}g$ 通过底夸克湮灭对希格斯加喷注产生有贡献，其包含 $\\mathcal{O}(ε^2)$ 或更高阶项的两圈振幅是构建红外相减后的三圈虚振幅所必需的。

Method: The amplitude is projected onto independent Lorentz tensor structures appropriate for the $H \rightarrow b\bar{b}g$ process. Using dimensional regularization with $d = 4 - 2ε$, the authors compute the two-loop Feynman diagrams in the massless limit (i.e., $m_b = 0$) and expand the resulting form factors up to higher powers in $ε$. This involves advanced techniques for multi-loop integration and handling of color and spinor algebra.

作者将振幅投影到适用于 $H \rightarrow b\bar{b}g$ 过程的独立洛伦兹张量结构上。利用维数正规化（$d = 4 - 2ε$），在无质量极限（即 $m_b = 0$）下计算两圈费曼图，并将所得形状因子展开至 $ε$ 的高阶项。该方法涉及多圈积分、色空间与旋量代数的先进处理技术。

Result: The paper provides analytic expressions for the two-loop form factors of the $H \rightarrow b\bar{b}g$ amplitude expanded up to $\\mathcal{O}(ε^2)$ (or higher), along with numerical evaluations at selected phase-space points demonstrating their behavior. These results constitute a necessary component for future calculations of three-loop virtual corrections to $b\\bar{b} \\rightarrow H + g$.
\n\n
本文给出了 $H \rightarrow b\bar{b}g$ 振幅两圈形状因子的解析表达式，展开至 $\\mathcal{O}(ε^2)$（或更高阶），并提供了若干相空间点上的数值结果以展示其行为。这些结果构成了未来计算 $b\\bar{b} \\rightarrow H + g$ 三圈虚修正所必需的组成部分。

Conclusion: The computation of $ε$-expanded two-loop amplitudes for $H \rightarrow b\\bar{b}g$ completes a critical step toward full three-loop QCD predictions for Higgs-plus-jet production via bottom-quark annihilation. 对 $H \rightarrow b\\bar{b}g$ 过程的 $ε$-展开两圈振幅的计算，完成了通过底夸克湮灭实现希格斯加喷注产生的完整三圈QCD预言的关键一步。这使得理论预言精度能够匹配当前及未来对撞机实验的数据精度。

Abstract: We compute the two-loop massless QCD corrections to the amplitude of Higgs boson decay to bottom quark pair and a gluon ($H \rightarrow b + \bar{b} + g$) in the higher powers of the dimensional regularization parameter $ε$. The calculation is performed by projecting the amplitude onto the appropriate Lorentz structures related to the process. We also show the numerical behaviour of the form factors for a few sample phase-space points. These amplitudes are necessary ingredients for computing the three-loop virtual corrections to bottom-quark annihilation to Higgs plus jet production at the hadron collisions.

TL;DR: The paper proposes a $B$-lattice flavor framework where fermion generations are elementary, but Yukawa hierarchies arise from $\mathbb{Z}_9$-graded chains of scalar subconstituents ("hops"). This structure explains quark/lepton mixing via charge differences, predicts neutrino mass ($m_3 \simeq 51$ meV), axion mass ($7$–$12\;\mu$eV), $\tan\beta \simeq 10$–$16$, and restores axion-photon coupling, all from two parameters ($\Lambda$, $\varepsilon = 14/75$).

本文提出一种 $B$-lattice flavor 框架：所有费米子代均为基本手征场，但通过由 $\mathbb{Z}_9$ 离散规范对称性分级的标量次组分（“hops”）链生成 Yukawa 耦合层级。该结构通过电荷差解释夸克/轻子混合，预言中微子质量（$m_3 \simeq 51$ meV）、轴子质量（$7$–$12\;\mu$eV）、$\tan\beta \simeq 10$–$16$，并恢复轴子-光子耦合，仅依赖两个参数（$\Lambda$ 和 $\varepsilon = 14/75$）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The Standard Model lacks an explanation for the hierarchical structure of fermion masses and mixing angles. The paper aims to address this by embedding flavor physics into a discrete gauge symmetry ($\mathbb{Z}_9$) that organizes Yukawa couplings through a compositeness-inspired lattice of scalar mediators, while simultaneously linking flavor to axion physics and neutrino mass generation.
\n\n
标准模型无法解释费米子质量与混合角的层级结构。本文旨在通过将味物理嵌入离散规范对称性（$\mathbb{Z}_9$），利用受复合性启发的标量中介粒子格点来组织 Yukawa 耦合，同时将味结构与轴子物理及中微子质量生成联系起来。

Method: The authors construct a $B$-lattice model where third-generation fermions couple directly to the Higgs (undressed, $Q=0$), while lighter generations acquire Yukawa couplings via sequential insertions of spin-$0$ subconstituents (“hops”) governed by a $\mathbb{Z}_9$ discrete gauge symmetry. The $\mathbb{Z}_9$ charge decomposes as $q_9 \mapsto (a,b)$, defining two hop types ($\alpha$, $\beta$). All mass scales follow a geometric “ninths ladder” $ \Lambda \varepsilon^{n/9} $. Mixing matrices (CKM/PMNS) emerge from charge differences $\Delta Q$, dressed by a universal Fritzsch–Xing phase shift of $\pm 1/9$. The DFSZ-II two-Higgs-doublet structure and generation-dependent Peccei–Quinn charges are incorporated to link to axion phenomenology.
\n\n
作者构建一个 $B$-lattice 模型：第三代费米子直接与希格斯耦合（未穿衣，$Q=0$），而较轻代通过由 $\mathbb{Z}_9$ 离散规范对称性控制的自旋为 0 的次组分（“hops”）链获得 Yukawa 耦合。$\mathbb{Z}_9$ 电荷分解为 $q_9 \mapsto (a,b)$，定义两类 hop（$\alpha$, $\beta$）。所有质量标度遵循几何“九分阶梯”$ \Lambda \varepsilon^{n/9} $。混合矩阵（CKM/PMNS）源于电荷差 $\Delta Q$，并附加普适的 Fritzsch–Xing 相位移动 $\pm 1/9$。模型结合 DFSZ-II 双希格斯二重态结构和代依赖的 Peccei–Quinn 电荷以关联轴子现象学。

Result: The framework successfully reproduces observed quark and lepton mixing patterns via $\Delta Q$-based exponents with a universal $\pm 1/9$ phase correction. It predicts: (i) a normal-hierarchy seesaw benchmark $m_3 \simeq 51$ meV; (ii) axion mass $m_a \sim 7$–$12\;\mu$eV; (iii) $\tan\beta \simeq 10$–$16$ from internal chain dynamics in DFSZ-II; and (iv) axion-photon coupling $C_{a\gamma} \simeq 0.6$–$1.0$, evading “invisible axion” suppression. Abstract: We calculate the Sudakov-limited maximum phase-space density associated with the linearly polarized gluon TMD coefficient $h_1^{\perp g}$ in the saturation region. This dipole result is a process-dependent TMD proxy, not a literal gluon number density. For the WW distribution, the deep-saturation tensor coefficient lacks the logarithmic enhancement needed for the Mueller saddle, so the maximum is pushed toward the saturation boundary. We also perform a numerical Collins-Soper evolution study and find that the $J_2$ Bessel weight in the tensor TMD definition reduces the resolved peak, giving $c_h^{\rm num}\simeq6.6$--$7.1$ for representative EIC scales.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation stems from inconsistencies in the conventional Breit-Wigner formalism when applied to realistic quantum mechanical scattering problems. Method: The authors solve the exactly solvable square well scattering problem to explicitly examine the behavior of phase shifts and resonance poles. They contrast the standard Breit-Wigner ansatz $\tanδ_{\rm BW} = Γ_1/(E_1 - E)$ with the exact solution and analyze the analytic structure of the S-matrix. Crucially, they invoke the antilinear $PT$ symmetry of the Schrödinger equation, which enforces that physical solutions must appear in complex conjugate energy pairs $E_{\mp} = E_2 \mp iΓ_2$, ensuring real expectation values and stable probability densities.

作者通过求解可精确求解的方势阱散射问题，显式考察相移和共振极点的行为。他们将标准Breit-Wigner假设 $\tanδ_{\rm BW} = Γ_1/(E_1 - E)$ 与精确解进行对比，并分析S矩阵的解析结构。关键在于，他们引入薛定谔方程的反线性 $PT$ 对称性，该对称性要求物理解必须以复共轭能量对 $E_{\mp} = E_2 \mp iΓ_2$ 的形式出现，从而保证期望值为实数且概率密度稳定。

Result: The analysis reveals that the Breit-Wigner form does not universally describe real-energy scattering amplitudes, $Γ_1$ can be negative, and the pole $E_{\rm BW} = E_1 - iΓ_1$ is not an eigenvalue of the Hamiltonian. Moreover, wavefunctions associated with such poles exhibit unphysical exponential growth in space. In contrast, the $PT$-symmetric framework yields a pair of complex-conjugate poles $E_2 \mp iΓ_2$, leading to a time-independent probability amplitude $|\psi(x,t)|^2$ that remains finite everywhere.
