分析表明，Breit-Wigner形式并非普遍适用于实能量散射振幅，$Γ_1$ 可为负，且极点 $E_{\rm BW} = E_1 - iΓ_1$ 并非哈密顿量的本征值。此外，与此类极点相关的波函数在空间中呈现非物理的指数增长。相比之下，$PT$ 对称框架给出一对复共轭极点 $E_2 \mp iΓ_2$，导致时间无关的概率幅 $|\psi(x,t)|^2$ 在全空间有限。该框架仅预言一个可观测共振，解决了BW方法中的双重性模糊和非物理行为。

Conclusion: The standard Breit-Wigner resonance paradigm is inadequate for describing true quantum mechanical resonances because it violates fundamental requirements of quantum theory, such as normalizability and unitary time evolution. A consistent description requires respecting the $PT$ symmetry of the underlying Schrödinger equation, which naturally leads to complex-conjugate energy pairs and a single physical resonance with well-behaved wavefunctions and positive-definite decay characteristics.
\n\n
标准的Breit-Wigner共振范式不足以描述真实的量子力学共振，因为它违反了量子理论的基本要求，如可归一化性和幺正时间演化。一致的描述必须尊重薛定谔方程的 $PT$ 对称性，这自然导致复共轭能量对，并给出具有良好行为波函数和正定衰变特性的单一物理共振。

Abstract: In the standard Breit-Wigner approach to scattering the phase shift is to have a form $\tanδ_{\rm BW} =Γ_1/(E_1-E)$ at a real energy resonance. This leads to complex energy poles in the scattering amplitude at $E_{\rm BW}=E_1-iΓ_1$, poles that are identified with unstable physical particles. By solving the square well scattering problem we identify some challenges to this approach. We find that setting $\tanδ_{\rm BW} =Γ_1/(E_1-E)$ is not always a good description of the real energy scattering amplitude, that $Γ_1$ can be negative, that $E_{\rm BW}$ is not in fact an energy eigenvalue (and thus not a physical particle), and that states that decay in energy possess spatial wave functions that unacceptably grow exponentially. All of this is resolved by noting that because of its antilinear $PT$ symmetry solutions to the square well Schrödinger equation appear in complex conjugate energy pairs $E_{\mp}=E_2\mp i Γ_2$ with $E_- \neq E_{\rm BW}$, doing so in a way that gives a time independent probability amplitude that neither grows nor decays in time or space, and leads to just one now observable physical resonance not two.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The strong $CP$ problem in QCD—why the observed neutron electric dipole moment implies an unnaturally small effective $θ$-angle—remains unresolved. Method: The authors employ the functional renormalization group (FRG) framework applied to a low-energy effective theory of QCD-like theories that includes both the standard chiral Lagrangian degrees of freedom and the $CP$-violating four-fermion operator $(\barψ ψ)(\barψ i γ_5 ψ)$. They consistently incorporate the running of the gauge coupling and analyze the renormalization group flow of the four-fermion coupling and the $θ$-parameter, especially across the chiral phase transition and in the presence of explicit chiral symmetry breaking from finite quark masses.
\n\n
作者采用泛函重整化群（FRG）框架，将其应用于包含标准手征拉氏量自由度和$CP$破坏四费米子算符$(\barψ ψ)(\barψ i γ_5 ψ)$的QCD类理论低能有效理论。他们一致地纳入规范耦合的跑动，并分析四费米子耦合与$θ$参数的重整化群流，特别是在手征相变附近以及存在由非零夸克质量引起的显式手征对称性破缺的情形下。

Result: The FRG analysis reveals that the $CP$-violating four-fermion coupling becomes relevant in the chirally broken phase when the running gauge coupling is taken into account—a result missed in fixed-coupling approximations. Moreover, for non-zero quark mass, the RG flow of the $θ$-parameter toward the infrared is strongly suppressed, indicating that UV-generated strong-$CP$ effects do not necessarily survive unattenuated at low energies.

FRG分析表明，当计入跑动的规范耦合时，$CP$破坏的四费米子耦合在手征对称性破缺相中变为相关算符——这一结果在固定耦合近似下会被遗漏。此外，对于非零夸克质量，$θ$参数向红外区的RG流被强烈压制，表明紫外区产生的强$CP$效应未必能无衰减地传递到低能区。

Conclusion: This work demonstrates that the interplay between dynamical chiral symmetry breaking, running gauge interactions, and explicit symmetry breaking from quark masses non-trivially modifies the infrared fate of $CP$-violating operators in QCD-like theories. Abstract: We study the low-energy properties of QCD-like theories in the presence of a $P$-odd and $U(1)$ axial breaking four-fermion operator $\left( \barψ ψ\right) \left( \barψ i γ_5 ψ\right)$. ### [54] [Energy-energy correlators inside single inclusive jets in heavy-ion collisions with CoLBT-hydro model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28788)
*Zhong Yang,Raghav Kunnawalkam Elayavalli,Xin-Nian Wang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: The paper presents an improved theoretical framework (CoLBT-hydro with a medium scale $Q_M = 2.0$ GeV) to compute the energy-energy correlator (EEC) inside jets in heavy-ion collisions, successfully reproducing CMS data and revealing how jet quenching and background subtraction affect EEC measurements. It also studies path-length dependence via leading/sub-leading jets and connects dijet rapidity gaps to jet-induced diffusion wakes.
\n\n
本文提出了一种改进的理论框架（引入介质能标 $Q_M = 2.0$ GeV 的 CoLBT-hydro），用于计算重离子碰撞中喷注内部的能量-能量关联器（EEC），成功复现了 CMS 实验数据，并揭示了喷注淬火和本底扣除对 EEC 测量的影响。文章还通过领头/次领头喷注研究了介质路径长度依赖性，并将双喷注快度间隔与喷注诱导的扩散尾迹联系起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Interpreting the energy-energy correlator (EEC) in heavy-ion collisions is difficult due to the need for a unified description of jet evolution across vacuum and in-medium regimes and robust background subtraction. Existing models lack a consistent separation between these regimes and often rely on ad hoc background treatments, limiting their predictive power for precision jet substructure observables like EEC.
\n\n
在重离子碰撞中解释能量-能量关联器（EEC）十分困难，因其需要统一描述喷注在真空与介质中的演化过程，并进行可靠的本底扣除。现有模型缺乏对这两个区域的一致划分，且通常依赖于临时性的本底处理方法，限制了其对 EEC 等精密喷注子结构观测量的预测能力。

Method: The authors use an updated CoLBT-hydro framework that introduces a medium scale $Q_M = 2.0$ GeV to demarcate vacuum-like and in-medium parton shower evolution. They implement two background subtraction schemes: (1) a theoretical subtraction based on the model’s own background estimate, and (2) the CMS mixed-event method applied directly in simulation. Jet samples are categorized by $p_T$ ranking (leading vs. sub-leading) and dijet rapidity gap to probe path-length and wake effects.
\n\n
作者采用更新后的 CoLBT-hydro 框架，引入介质能标 $Q_M = 2.0$ GeV 来划分类真空与介质中的部分子簇射演化。他们实现了两种本底扣除方案：(1) 基于模型自身背景估计的理论扣除；(2) 在模拟中直接应用 CMS 的混合事例方法。喷注样本按 $p_T$ 排序（领头与次领头）及双喷注快度间隔分类，以探测路径长度和尾迹效应。

Result: The simulation with theoretical background subtraction successfully reproduces the CMS in-jet EEC measurement in Pb-Pb collisions. Decomposition shows significant medium modification at angular scales $\\theta \\sim 0.1$–$1$. Results using the CMS mixed-event subtraction agree with the theoretical method, validating the experimental procedure. Sub-leading jets exhibit stronger medium modification than leading jets, indicating path-length dependence. The EEC of leading jets varies with dijet rapidity gap, suggesting sensitivity to the jet-induced diffusion wake.
\n\n
采用理论本底扣除的模拟成功复现了 CMS 在 Pb-Pb 碰撞中测得的喷注内 EEC。成分分解表明在角尺度 $\\theta \\sim 0.1$–$1$ 处存在显著的介质修正。使用 CMS 混合事例扣除法的结果与理论方法一致，验证了实验流程。次领头喷注比领头喷注表现出更强的介质修正，表明其具有路径长度依赖性。领头喷注的 EEC 随双喷注快度间隔变化，暗示其对喷注诱导的扩散尾迹敏感。

Conclusion: Introducing a physical medium scale $Q_M$ enables a more self-consistent treatment of jet evolution in heavy-ion collisions. The EEC is confirmed as a powerful probe of jet quenching dynamics, sensitive to both in-medium path length and collective response (e.g., diffusion wake). The agreement between theoretical and experimental background subtraction methods enhances confidence in interpreting jet substructure measurements in QGP.
\n\n
引入物理介质能标 $Q_M$ 使得重离子碰撞中喷注演化的处理更加自洽。EEC 被证实是探测喷注淬火动力学的有力工具，对介质中的路径长度和集体响应（如扩散尾迹）均敏感。理论与实验本底扣除方法的一致性增强了对 QGP 中喷注子结构测量解释的信心。

Abstract: The energy-energy correlator (EEC) inside jets is a sensitive observable for studying jet modification in the quark-gluon plasma (QGP). However, its interpretation in heavy-ion collisions remains challenging, requiring a consistent understanding of jet evolution across multiple dynamical scales together with a proper treatment of the background subtraction. In this work, we employ an updated CoLBT-hydro framework in which a medium scale $Q_M$ = 2.0 GeV is introduced to separate the vacuum and in-medium stages of the parton shower, enabling a more self-consistent treatment of jet evolution. Using a theoretical background subtraction within the model, the resulting simulation reproduces the recent CMS measurement of the in-jet EEC, and through a decomposition of different contributions, highlights the impact of medium modification on the observable. To further validate the experimental procedure, we also implement the CMS mixed-event background-subtraction method directly in the simulation and find the results are consistent with that obtained with the theoretical background subtraction. Using $p_T$-ranked jets in each event, we further investigate the dependence of medium modification on the in-medium path length, reflected in the different EECs of leading and sub-leading jets. Finally, we explore the dependence of the leading-jet EEC on the dijet rapidity gap as a signal of the jet-induced diffusion wake.

</details>

### [55] [Gravitational helicity in connection variables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27493)

本文利用协变相空间方法和Ashtekar变量，将引力螺旋度构建为广义相对论中内对偶对称性对应的守恒Noether荷，并进一步将其与Nieh-Yan拓扑项联系起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation is to rigorously define gravitational helicity—a quantity analogous to magnetic helicity in electromagnetism—as a conserved charge arising from a duality symmetry in general relativity, and to uncover its topological nature by connecting it to known topological invariants like the Nieh-Yan term.

动机在于严格定义引力螺旋度——一种类似于电磁学中磁螺旋度的量——作为广义相对论中对偶对称性所产生的守恒荷，并通过将其与Nieh-Yan项等已知拓扑不变量联系起来揭示其拓扑本质。

Method: The authors use the covariant phase space formalism of general relativity formulated in connection variables. By switching to complex self-dual Ashtekar variables, this duality becomes a $U(1)$ phase rotation, allowing them to apply Noether’s theorem to derive the conserved helicity charge. They then re-express this charge in terms of real variables to relate it to the Nieh-Yan topological term.

作者采用以联络变量表述的广义相对论协变相空间形式体系，构造辛形式并识别出内Hodge对偶变换。通过转换到复自对偶Ashtekar变量，该对偶变为一个$U(1)$相位旋转，从而可应用Noether定理导出守恒的螺旋度荷。随后，他们将该荷用实变量重新表达，以将其与Nieh-Yan拓扑项关联。

Result: The paper successfully identifies gravitational helicity as the Noether charge of the internal duality symmetry, shows that in self-dual Ashtekar variables the symmetry is a manifest $U(1)$, and demonstrates that the helicity equals (up to boundary terms) the Nieh-Yan topological invariant when written in real variables.

本文成功将引力螺旋度识别为内对偶对称性的Noether荷，表明在自对偶Ashtekar变量下该对称性表现为明显的$U(1)$对称性，并证明当用实变量表达时，该螺旋度（在边界项范围内）等于Nieh-Yan拓扑不变量。

Conclusion: Gravitational helicity is a well-defined conserved quantity tied to an internal duality symmetry of general relativity, and its equivalence to the Nieh-Yan term reveals a deep connection between gravitational dynamics and topology in the first-order formulation of gravity.

引力螺旋度是一个与广义相对论内对偶对称性相关的良好定义的守恒量，其与Nieh-Yan项的等价性揭示了在引力的一阶形式体系中动力学与拓扑之间的深刻联系。

Abstract: We study the gravitational helicity using the covariant phase space method. Starting from the covariant phase space of general relativity expressed in terms of connection variables, we construct the symplectic form and identify a duality transformation based on the internal Hodge dual. To make the symmetry manifest, we employ the complex self-dual Ashtekar variables, where the duality becomes a simple $U(1)$ phase rotation. The Noether charge associated with this duality yields a conserved quantity which is interpreted as the gravitational helicity. Remarkably, this helicity can be related to the Nieh-Yan topological term, when expressed in terms of the real variables, establishing a direct link between the gravitational helicity and a topological invariant.

### [56] [Constraining Gravitational Wave Memory with Hierarchical Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27500)
*Keefe Mitman,Maximiliano Isi,Will M. Farr*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: This paper uses hierarchical Bayesian inference on the GWTC-4.0 catalog to search for evidence of the gravitational wave null memory effect—a nonlinear prediction of general relativity—finding a memory enhancement factor consistent with GR ($1$) at $0.32^{+6.30}_{-5.12}$, and forecasting $\sim$2,500 detections needed for a $1σ$ detection.

本文利用 GWTC-4.0 目录中的双黑洞观测数据，采用 hierarchical Bayesian inference 方法搜寻引力波零记忆效应（广义相对论的一个非线性预言）的证据，测得记忆增强因子为 $0.32^{+6.30}_{-5.12}$，与 GR 预言值 $1$ 一致，并预测约需 $\sim$2,500 次探测才能在 $1σ$ 水平上排除零值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Previous analyses of the gravitational wave memory effect relied on Bayes factors, which are sensitive to prior choices and can yield misleading results when naively combined across many events. To overcome these limitations and robustly assess evidence for the nonlinear memory effect in current LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA (LVK) data, the authors adopt hierarchical Bayesian inference, which avoids the pitfalls of multiplying Bayes factors.
\n\n
此前对引力波记忆效应的分析依赖于 Bayes factors，该方法对先验选择敏感，且在多个事件中简单相乘时可能导致错误结论。为克服这些局限性，并在当前 LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA（LVK）数据中稳健地评估非线性记忆效应的证据，作者采用了 hierarchical Bayesian inference 方法，避免了 Bayes factors 相乘带来的问题。

Method: The authors perform hierarchical Bayesian inference using the GWTC-4.0 catalog of binary black hole mergers. They model the memory contribution to the strain waveform via a memory enhancement factor $\\kappa$, which multiplies the supermomentum flux term predicted by GR. Instead of computing individual Bayes factors per event and multiplying them, they infer the population-level posterior distribution of $\\kappa$, assuming it is constant across all events.
\n\n
作者利用 GWTC-4.0 双黑洞并合目录，采用 hierarchical Bayesian inference 方法。他们通过一个记忆增强因子 $\\kappa$ 对应变波形中的记忆贡献进行建模，该因子乘以 GR 预言的 supermomentum flux 项。不同于对每个事件单独计算 Bayes factors 并相乘，他们假设 $\\kappa$ 在所有事件中恒定，直接推断其总体后验分布。

Result: The analysis yields a constraint on the memory enhancement factor of $\\kappa = 0.32^{+6.30}_{-5.12}$ at the 68% credible level, consistent with the GR prediction $\\kappa = 1$. The result shows no significant evidence for or against the memory effect in current data. A forecast indicates that approximately 2,500 detections will be required to constrain $\\kappa$ away from zero at $1σ$ significance.
\n\n
分析得出记忆增强因子的约束为 $\\kappa = 0.32^{+6.30}_{-5.12}$（68% 置信区间），与 GR 预言值 $\\kappa = 1$ 一致。当前数据未显示出记忆效应的显著证据。预测表明，约需 2,500 次探测才能在 $1σ$ 显著性水平上将 $\\kappa$ 与零值区分开。

Conclusion: Hierarchical Bayesian inference provides a more robust framework than Bayes-factor multiplication for detecting subtle, cumulative effects like the gravitational wave memory. While current LVK data are insufficient to claim detection, the methodology confirms consistency with GR and quantifies the observational threshold needed for future confirmation.
\n\n
Hierarchical Bayesian inference 为探测引力波记忆这类微弱而累积性的效应提供了比 Bayes-factor 相乘更稳健的框架。尽管当前 LVK 数据尚不足以宣称探测到记忆效应，但该方法验证了与 GR 的一致性，并量化了未来实现确认所需的观测阈值。

Abstract: With the multitude of gravitational wave observations that have been made in the past ten years, probing the dynamical and nonlinear nature of strong gravity is becoming more and more feasible. One promising way to test the nonlinear nature of Einstein's theory of general relativity (GR) is through the gravitational wave null memory effect: a nonlinear prediction of GR which corresponds to initially comoving observers being permanently displaced due to a burst of gravitational radiation. Previous studies have shown that, while it is unlikely that the memory effect will be observed in a single event by the LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA (LVK) detectors, evidence for memory in the population of LVK events should be attainable after $\sim$2,000 gravitational wave detections. These works, however, largely relied on Bayes factors to perform their memory analyses: an approach that can depend sensitively on the analysis priors and, when naively multiplied across many events, can even favor incorrect conclusions. In this work, using the GWTC-4.0 catalog of binary black hole observations, we instead perform hierarchical Bayesian inference -- which is not subject to the issues associated with Bayes factors -- to measure the evidence for memory in current LVK observations. We find that we can constrain what we call the memory enhancement factor -- the constant appearing in front of the contribution to the strain from the supermomentum flux -- to $0.32^{+6.30}_{-5.12}$ (with $\pm$ values denoting the 68% credible interval), consistent with its GR value of 1. We forecast that $\sim$2,500 detections will be needed to constrain the memory enhancement factor away from zero at the $1σ$ level.

</details>


### [57] [Charting causal set configuration space with graph observables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27514)
*Astrid Eichhorn,Harald Mack,Kim Tuyen Le,Fabian Wagner*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: This paper explores nine distinct classes of causal sets—both manifoldlike (with inhomogeneous Ricci curvature, trivial and nontrivial topology) and non-manifoldlike (e.g., lattices, layered orders, Lorentzian quasicrystals)—as a testbed for graph-theoretic observables. It identifies three low-fluctuation observables—link degree distribution, eigenvalues of the graph Laplacian of the symmetrized Hasse diagram, and abundance of causal intervals—that effectively distinguish between these classes, outperforming costly continuum-inspired curvature observables.

本文研究了九类不同的因果集（causal sets）——包括具有非均匀Ricci曲率的流形类（manifoldlike）因果集（拓扑平凡与非平凡）以及非流形类（non-manifoldlike）因果集（如格点、分层序、Lorentzian准晶体）——作为图论可观测量的测试平台。研究发现三种涨落较小的可观测量——链度分布（link degree distribution）、对称化Hasse图的图拉普拉斯算子（graph Laplacian）本征值，以及新近提出的因果区间丰度（abundance of causal intervals）——能有效区分这些类别，优于计算昂贵且涨落大的连续几何启发的曲率不变量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The vast configuration space of causal sets lacks systematic characterization. Traditional continuum-inspired observables (e.g., curvature invariants) suffer from large statistical fluctuations and high computational cost, limiting their utility in distinguishing causal set classes. There is a need for robust, computationally efficient observables that can reliably differentiate between manifoldlike and non-manifoldlike causal sets, including those expected to become manifoldlike only after coarse-graining.
\n\n
因果集的构型空间极为庞大，缺乏系统性刻画。传统的连续几何启发的可观测量（如曲率不变量）存在显著统计涨落和高昂计算成本，难以有效区分不同因果集类别。因此亟需稳健且计算高效的可观测量，以可靠地区分流形类与非流形类因果集，包括那些仅在适当粗粒化（coarse-graining）后才呈现流形行为的因果集。

Method: The authors construct and analyze nine distinct ensembles of causal sets: (i) manifoldlike sprinklings into spacetimes with inhomogeneous Ricci curvature (both topologically trivial $\\mathbb{R}^d$ and nontrivial $S^1 \\times \\mathbb{R}^{d-1}$), (ii) explicitly non-manifoldlike causal sets such as regular lattices, layered orders, and Lorentzian quasicrystals, and (iii) intermediate cases conjectured to flow to manifoldlike behavior under coarse-graining. They evaluate a suite of graph-theoretic observables—both local (subgraph statistics like link degree distribution) and global (e.g., spectrum of the graph Laplacian of the symmetrized Hasse diagram)—and compare their performance against traditional geometric observables. Key emphasis is placed on observables with small ensemble fluctuations.
\n\n
作者构建并分析了九类不同的因果集系综：(i) 向具有非均匀Ricci曲率的时空（包括拓扑平凡的$\\mathbb{R}^d$和非平凡的$S^1 \\times \\mathbb{R}^{d-1}$）中进行sprinkling生成的流形类因果集；(ii) 显式非流形类因果集，如规则格点、分层序和Lorentzian准晶体；(iii) 推测在粗粒化下会流向流形行为的中间情形。他们评估了一系列图论可观测量——包括局域的（如链度分布等子图统计量）和全局的（如对称化Hasse图的图拉普拉斯算子谱），并与传统几何可观测量进行比较，重点关注系综涨落小的可观测量。

Result: Three graph observables exhibit low variance across most causal set ensembles and successfully distinguish between all nine classes: (1) the link degree distribution $P(k)$, which captures local connectivity; (2) the eigenvalue spectrum $\\{\\lambda_i\\}$ of the graph Laplacian $\\Delta = D - A$ (where $D$ is the degree matrix and $A$ the adjacency matrix of the symmetrized Hasse diagram); and (3) the abundance of causal intervals $n_k$, defined as the number of pairs of elements with exactly $k$ elements in their causal interval. These outperform curvature-based observables, which show large fluctuations and poor discriminative power.
\n\n
三类图可观测量在大多数因果集系综中表现出低方差，并成功区分全部九类：(1) 链度分布$P(k)$，刻画局域连通性；(2) 对称化Hasse图的图拉普拉斯算子$\\Delta = D - A$（其中$D$为度矩阵，$A$为邻接矩阵）的本征值谱$\\{\\lambda_i\\}$；(3) 因果区间丰度$n_k$，定义为因果区间内恰好包含$k$个元素的元素对数目。这些可观测量优于基于曲率的量，后者涨落大且区分能力弱。

Conclusion: Graph-theoretic observables—particularly those with small statistical fluctuations—provide a powerful and computationally efficient toolkit for characterizing and classifying causal sets. The identified observables (link degree distribution, Laplacian spectrum, and causal interval abundance) serve as effective order parameters across the causal set configuration space, enabling discrimination between manifoldlike, non-manifoldlike, and coarse-graining-transitional classes. This paves the way for systematic exploration of the causal set landscape using discrete, intrinsic tools rather than continuum approximations.
\n\n
图论可观测量——尤其是统计涨落小的那些——为刻画和分类因果集提供了强大且计算高效的工具集。所识别的可观测量（链度分布、拉普拉斯谱和因果区间丰度）可作为因果集构型空间中的有效序参量，能够区分流形类、非流形类以及粗粒化过渡类因果集。这为使用离散内禀工具（而非连续近似）系统探索因果集景观铺平了道路。

Abstract: The configuration space of causal sets is vast. It is a critical goal to map out this space. Here, we take a practical step towards this goal. We investigate nine classes of causal sets, most of them not studied before. These include manifoldlike causal sets with inhomogeneous Ricci curvature, both topologically trivial and nontrivial. We also study classes of non-manifoldlike causal sets, including lattices, layered orders as well as Lorentzian quasicrystals. Finally, we study classes of causal sets that are not manifoldlike, but are expected to become manifoldlike under a suitable coarse-graining process. We use this broad range of distinct classes of causal sets as a testbed for observables. Rather than focusing on continuum-geometry inspired observables, such as curvature invariants, which often exhibit large fluctuations and are computationally very expensive, we focus on graph observables, including some observables that constitute subgraph statistics and some that are global. We find that three observables, namely the link degree distribution, the eigenvalues of the graph Laplacian of the symmetrized Hasse diagram and the recently proposed abundance of causal intervals, can distinguish between the distinct classes of causal sets. This is made possible by the small fluctuations that these observables have in most classes.

</details>


### [58] [Stationary generalizations for the vacuum ring wormhole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27600)
*Mikhail S. Volkov*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: The paper constructs non-perturbative, stationary, reflection-symmetric generalizations of the static ring wormhole (the zero-mass limit of Kerr). These solutions interpolate between a non-relativistic $M \sim J^2$ regime at slow rotation and a Regge-like $J = M^2$ behavior at fast rotation.   <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation is to explore the space of stationary, asymptotically flat vacuum (or scalarized) solutions beyond the Kerr family that possess nontrivial topology (wormholes). The static ring wormhole serves as a seed solution, and the goal is to find its rotating counterparts that are symmetric across the throat, thereby providing new theoretical laboratories to study the interplay between topology, rotation, and gravity, and to investigate how closely non-Kerr objects can mimic black hole observables like the extremal Kerr geometry.
\n\n
动机在于探索 Kerr 族之外的、具有非平凡拓扑（虫洞）的定态渐近平直真空（或标量化）解空间。静态环虫洞作为种子解，目标是找到其关于喉部对称的旋转对应物，从而为研究拓扑、旋转与引力之间的相互作用提供新的理论平台，并探究非 Kerr 天体能在多大程度上“模仿”黑洞观测量（如极端 Kerr 几何）。

Method: The method involves solving the vacuum Ernst equations for stationary, axisymmetric spacetimes under the imposed symmetry of reflection invariance across the wormhole throat. The authors move beyond previous perturbative (slow-rotation) analyses by employing a numerical framework to construct fully non-perturbative solutions. They then analyze the global properties, the $M$-$J$ relation, and the geometric limits of these solutions. Finally, they apply a “scalarization” procedure, promoting the vacuum solutions to solutions of Einstein-scalar theory with a phantom scalar field to resolve the ring singularity.
\n\n
方法涉及在虫洞喉部反射不变性的约束下，求解定态轴对称时空的真空 Ernst 方程。作者超越了先前的微扰（慢旋转）分析，采用数值框架来构建完全非微扰的解。随后，他们分析了这些解的整体性质、$M$-$J$ 关系及几何极限。最后，他们应用一种“标量化”（scalarization）程序，将真空解提升为带有幻影标量场的 Einstein-标量理论的解，以消除环奇点。

Result: The key results are: (1) The construction of non-perturbative, stationary, reflection-symmetric wormhole solutions. (2) The identification of two distinct rotation regimes: a slow-rotation regime with $M \sim J^2$ and a fast-rotation regime with the Regge relation $J = M^2$. (3) The discovery of a double-scaling limit where the static ring size vanishes as $J \to \infty$, leading to a bounded $M, J$ and a geometry that approaches extremal Kerr. (4) The demonstration that the distributional curvature singularity on the ring can be removed by coupling the geometry to a phantom scalar field, yielding regular wormhole solutions.

主要结果包括：(1) 构建了非微扰、定态、反射对称的虫洞解。(2) 识别出两个不同的旋转区域：慢旋转区满足 $M \sim J^2$，快旋转区满足 Regge 关系 $J = M^2$。(3) 发现了一个双重缩放极限，在此极限下静态环尺寸随 $J \to \infty$ 而消失，导致 $M, J$ 有界，且几何趋近于极端 Kerr 解。(4) 证明了通过将几何与幻影标量场耦合，可以消除环上的分布型曲率奇点，从而得到正则虫洞解。

Conclusion: The paper concludes that a rich family of non-Kerr, topologically nontrivial wormhole solutions exists, which can closely mimic the geometry of an extremal Kerr black hole in a specific physical limit. This demonstrates that the Kerr metric is not unique in reproducing certain strong-field gravitational features. Furthermore, the inherent singularity of the vacuum wormhole can be resolved through a simple scalarization mechanism, suggesting a viable path to constructing physically plausible, regular, rotating wormhole spacetimes.

本文得出结论：存在一个丰富的非 Kerr、拓扑非平凡的虫洞解族，其在特定物理极限下能紧密“模仿”极端 Kerr 黑洞的几何。这表明 Kerr 度规在复现某些强场引力特征方面并非唯一。此外，真空虫洞固有的奇点可通过简单的标量化机制得以解决，为构建物理上合理的、正则的、旋转虫洞时空提供了一条可行路径。

Abstract: The ring wormhole is the zero-mass limit of the Kerr metric. Its geometry is locally flat, but the topology is nontrivial, with a throat connecting two asymptotic regions and a distributional curvature singularity on the ring encircling the throat. We construct stationary generalizations of this static wormhole that are different from Kerr and invariant under reflections across the wormhole throat. The problem reduces to solving the vacuum Ernst equations subject to the corresponding symmetry conditions. The slowly rotating perturbative solutions were constructed previously, while we now present a detailed analysis of non-perturbative solutions obtained within a numerical framework. For slow rotation, they exhibit the non-relativistic relation $M\sim J^2$ between the mass and angular momentum, which transforms into the Regge relation $J=M^2$ in the fast-rotation regime, when $J\to\infty$ and the ring is stretched without bound by the centrifugal force. However, if the ring size in the static limit is sent to zero at the same time, then $M$ and $J$ remain bounded as the throat linear velocity approaches unity. The wormhole geometry then approaches the extremal Kerr solution, thus ``mimicking'' it. The wormholes carry a curvature singularity at the ring, but this can be removed by via simple ``scalarization'' procedure that promotes the vacuum solutions to regular wormholes with a phantom scalar field.

</details>

Main category: gr-qc

  <summary>Details</summary>

动机是在标准爱因斯坦引力框架内构建正则黑洞时空，无需引入奇异物质或高阶导数引力修正，同时通过一个极小耦合的非线性电动力学暗区自然地在大尺度上引入洛伦兹对称性破缺。

Method: The authors employ a minimally coupled matter sector described by nonlinear electrodynamics, choosing specific Lagrangians that yield energy-momentum tensors supporting regular geometries. They solve the Einstein equations with these sources to obtain static, spherically symmetric metrics that transition from a de Sitter-like core ($r \\to 0$) to an asymptotic conical deficit ($g_{tt} \\sim -1 + 8\\pi G \\mu - 2GM/r$, with $\\mu$ as effective tension), thereby breaking Lorentz invariance asymptotically.
\n\n
作者采用由非线性电动力学描述的极小耦合物质区，选取特定拉格朗日量以产生支持正则几何的能量-动量张量。他们求解带有此类源的爱因斯坦方程，获得静态球对称度规，该度规从类de Sitter核心（$r \\to 0$）过渡到具有锥形亏缺的渐近结构（$g_{tt} \\sim -1 + 8\\pi G \\mu - 2GM/r$，其中$\\mu$为有效张力），从而在渐近处破缺洛伦兹不变性。

Result: The framework successfully reproduces Lorentz-violating versions of Schwarzschild and Reissner-Nordström black holes, as well as regular black hole solutions such as Bardeen and Hayward types, all arising from minimally coupled nonlinear electrodynamics without curvature-squared terms or nonminimal couplings.

该框架成功复现了Schwarzschild和Reissner-Nordström黑洞的洛伦兹破缺版本，以及Bardeen和Hayward等类型的正则黑洞解，所有这些都源于极小耦合的非线性电动力学，无需曲率平方项或非极小耦合。

Abstract: We introduce a minimally coupled dark sector based on a nonlinear electrodynamics to generate regular spacetime textures that interpolate from a regular core to an asymptotic Lorentz-violating conical geometry. This construction provides a simple mechanism, in Einstein gravity with minimally coupled matter, for obtaining Lorentz-violating Schwarzschild and Reissner-Nordström black holes. The framework further allows us to construct Lorentz-violating regular black holes, including the Bardeen and Hayward black holes, as well as a class of electrically-charged regular black holes.

*V. K. Oikonomou*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: This paper constructs viable Einstein-Gauss-Bonnet inflationary models compatible with ACT and GW170817 observations by using an inverse reconstruction method to determine the scalar potential and Gauss-Bonnet coupling function that yield a specified tensor-to-scalar ratio $r$. Four observationally consistent models are presented.
\n\n
本文利用逆向重构方法构建了与ACT和GW170817观测兼容的Einstein-Gauss-Bonnet暴胀模型，通过给定张量标量比 $r$ 来确定相应的标量势和Gauss-Bonnet耦合函数，并提出了四个通过所有观测检验的可行模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation stems from the need to reconcile modified gravity theories—specifically Einstein-Gauss-Bonnet gravity—with high-precision cosmological data such as those from the Atacama Cosmology Telescope (ACT) and the gravitational wave event GW170817. Standard single-field slow-roll inflation in General Relativity faces tension with certain observational bounds, prompting exploration of higher-curvature corrections like the Gauss-Bonnet term coupled to a scalar field. However, constructing viable models that satisfy all observational constraints (e.g., scalar amplitude $A_s$, spectral index $n_s$, and tensor-to-scalar ratio $r$) is non-trivial due to the modified perturbation dynamics in Einstein-Gauss-Bonnet theories.
\n\n
动机源于需要将修改引力理论（特别是Einstein-Gauss-Bonnet引力）与高精度宇宙学数据（如Atacama Cosmology Telescope (ACT) 和引力波事件GW170817的数据）相协调。广义相对论中的标准单场慢滚暴胀模型在某些观测限制下存在张力，促使人们探索与标量场耦合的高阶曲率修正项（如Gauss-Bonnet项）。然而，在Einstein-Gauss-Bonnet理论中，由于扰动动力学被修改，构造满足所有观测约束（如标量扰动振幅 $A_s$、谱指数 $n_s$ 和张量标量比 $r$）的可行模型具有非平凡性。

Method: The authors employ an inverse reconstruction technique: instead of assuming a specific form for the scalar potential $V(\phi)$ or the Gauss-Bonnet coupling function $\xi(\phi)$, they start from a desired value of the tensor-to-scalar ratio $r$ and use consistency relations derived from the Einstein-Gauss-Bonnet field equations to reconstruct $V(\phi)$ and $\xi(\phi)$. The formalism incorporates the modified Friedmann and perturbation equations, ensuring compatibility with the speed of gravitational waves (as constrained by GW170817, i.e., $c_T^2 = 1$). The reconstructed models are then tested against observational constraints on $A_s$, $n_s$, and $r$ from ACT and other CMB experiments.
\n\n
作者采用了一种逆向重构技术：不预先假设标量势 $V(\phi)$ 或Gauss-Bonnet耦合函数 $\xi(\phi)$ 的具体形式，而是从一个给定的张量标量比 $r$ 出发，利用从Einstein-Gauss-Bonnet场方程导出的一致性关系来重构 $V(\phi)$ 和 $\xi(\phi)$。该形式体系包含了修正的Friedmann方程和扰动方程，并确保引力波速度满足GW170817的限制（即 $c_T^2 = 1$）。随后，重构出的模型被用于检验其是否满足ACT及其他CMB实验对标量振幅 $A_s$、谱指数 $n_s$ 和 $r$ 的观测约束。

Result: The paper successfully identifies four distinct Einstein-Gauss-Bonnet inflationary models that simultaneously satisfy: (i) the constraint $c_T^2 = 1$ from GW170817, (ii) ACT-compatible values of $n_s$ and $r$, and (iii) the correct amplitude of scalar perturbations $A_s \approx 2.1 \times 10^{-9}$. This demonstrates that viable inflation can occur in Einstein-Gauss-Bonnet gravity without fine-tuning, provided the coupling functions are appropriately chosen via the reconstruction method.

论文成功找到了四个不同的Einstein-Gauss-Bonnet暴胀模型，它们同时满足：(i) GW170817对引力波速度的约束 $c_T^2 = 1$；(ii) 与ACT观测兼容的 $n_s$ 和 $r$ 值；(iii) 正确的标量扰动振幅 $A_s \approx 2.1 \times 10^{-9}$。这表明，只要通过重构方法适当选择耦合函数，Einstein-Gauss-Bonnet引力中无需精细调节即可实现可行的暴胀。

Conclusion: Einstein-Gauss-Bonnet inflation remains a phenomenologically viable framework when confronted with modern multi-messenger and CMB data. The inverse reconstruction approach provides a powerful tool to build theoretically consistent and observationally allowed models, circumventing the difficulties of forward model-building in higher-curvature gravity.   <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation stems from the need to test the Kerr hypothesis and the no-hair theorem in strong gravitational fields using observable signatures beyond classical geodesic motion. The authors focus on the latter to enable rigorous wave-based calculations, as wave absorption encodes information about spacetime structure inaccessible to purely geometric (null geodesic) probes. Finite-frequency wave effects offer complementary sensitivity to metric deformations, especially those affecting radial propagation without altering asymptotic or high-frequency limits.

动机源于在强引力场中检验Kerr假设和无毛定理的需求，需超越经典测地线运动的可观测信号。尽管Johannsen度规为偏离Kerr几何提供了灵活的参数化，但仅特定子类保留了关键的可积性性质，如Carter常数守恒和Klein-Gordon可分离性。作者聚焦于后者，以实现严格的基于波动的计算，因为波吸收编码了纯几何（零测地线）探针无法获取的时空结构信息。有限频率的波动效应为度规形变提供了互补的敏感性，特别是那些影响径向传播但不改变渐近或高频极限的形变。

Method: The authors restrict to a subclass of the asymptotically flat Johannsen metric where the Klein-Gordon equation for a massless scalar field is separable. A partial wave expansion is constructed, focusing on the leading-order deformation sectors $A_1(r)$, $A_2(r)$, and $A_5(r)$. The radial equation is analyzed to distinguish deformations that modify the effective radial size function $X(r) = \Delta - a^2(1 - A_2)\sin^2\theta$ (evaluated on the equatorial plane) from those entering only the kinetic term (e.g., $A_5$). Absorption cross sections are computed across frequencies, and superradiant scattering is examined by evaluating horizon boundary conditions and the modified horizon angular velocity $\Omega_H = a / X(r_+)$.

作者限制在渐近平直Johannsen度规的一个子类中，其中无质量标量场的Klein-Gordon方程可分离。他们施加可分离性条件以约束形变函数，随后将标量平面波分解为自旋加权球状谐函数，得到解耦的角向和径向常微分方程。构建了分波展开，聚焦于前导阶形变项 $A_1(r)$、$A_2(r)$ 和 $A_5(r)$。通过分析径向方程，区分两类形变：一类修改有效径向尺度函数 $X(r) = \Delta - a^2(1 - A_2)\sin^2\theta$（在赤道面上取值），另一类仅进入动能项（如 $A_5$）。计算了全频段的吸收截面，并通过视界边界条件及修正的视界角速度 $\Omega_H = a / X(r_+)$ 分析了超辐射散射。

Result: The study finds that deformations modifying $X(r)$—specifically through $A_1$ and $A_2$—alter both the low-frequency absorption cross section (violating the universal area law $\sigma_{\\text{abs}} \\to A_H$ as $\\omega \\to 0$) and the high-frequency geometric optics limit (changing the null geodesic capture cross section). These results demonstrate that finite-frequency absorption serves as a unique wave-optics observable sensitive to radial metric structure beyond classical limits.

研究发现，通过 $A_1$ 和 $A_2$ 修改 $X(r)$ 的形变会同时改变低频吸收截面（破坏低频普适面积律 $\sigma_{\\text{abs}} \\to A_H$ 当 $\\omega \\to 0$）和高频几何光学极限（改变零测地线捕获截面）。相比之下，纯 $A_5(r)$ 形变虽不改变主导阶零测地线捕获截面，但由于其对径向波传播的影响，在有限频率吸收谱中仍产生可观测偏差。非轴向入射打破了共转与反转入射模之间的简并，而 $X(r_+)$ 的变化会移动视界角速度 $\Omega_H$，从而改变超辐射条件 $\\omega < m \\Omega_H$。这些结果表明，有限频率吸收作为一种独特的波动光学可观测量，对超越经典极限的径向度规结构具有敏感性。

Conclusion: Finite-frequency scalar wave absorption provides a powerful new diagnostic for probing deviations from Kerr geometry in the strong-field regime, particularly for deformations that do not affect classical observables like horizon area or photon ring size. By leveraging Klein-Gordon separability in a controlled subclass of Johannsen spacetimes, the work establishes a rigorous wave-scattering framework that isolates distinct physical effects of metric deformations. This approach complements traditional tests based on geodesic motion and offers enhanced sensitivity to the radial propagation sector of black hole spacetimes, paving the way for future observational tests with gravitational-wave and electromagnetic wave data.

有限频率标量波吸收为探测强场区域中偏离Kerr几何的形变提供了一种强有力的新诊断工具，尤其适用于那些不影响视界面积或光子环尺寸等经典可观测量的形变。通过在Johannsen时空的一个可控子类中利用Klein-Gordon可分离性，本工作建立了一个严格的波散射框架，能够分离度规形变的不同物理效应。该方法补充了基于测地线运动的传统检验，并增强了对黑洞时空径向传播结构的敏感性，为未来利用引力波和电磁波数据进行观测检验铺平了道路。

Abstract: Johannsen metric is a natural and significant generalization of the Kerr metric, representing the most general stationary, axisymmetric spacetime that preserves the Carter constant of motion. The former modify the low-frequency area law, the high-frequency null-capture cross section, and the finite-frequency absorption spectra, whereas a pure $A_5$ deformation leaves the leading null-capture observable unchanged while remaining detectable in wave propagation. We further examine off-axis incidence, co-/counter-rotating contributions, and superradiant modes, where changes in $X(r_+)$ shift the horizon angular velocity and hence the superradiant threshold. Method: The authors impose spherical symmetry and conformal flatness (via $C_{\\mu\\nu\\rho\\sigma} = 0$, i.e., vanishing Weyl tensor) on the spacetime metric, which restricts the line element to $ds^2 = \\Omega^2(t,r)(-dt^2 + dr^2 + r^2 d\\Omega^2)$. Einstein’s equations are solved analytically under two scenarios: (i) non-dissipative ($q^\\mu = 0$), yielding separable $\\Omega(t,r) = T(t)R(r)$; (ii) self-similar collapse with homothetic Killing vector, requiring $q^\\mu \\neq 0$. Abstract: We investigate the gravitational collapse of a massive scalar field in a conformally flat, spherically symmetric spacetime within general relativity. The collapsing matter distribution is modeled using a minimally coupled homogeneous scalar field together with both perfect fluid and dissipative matter sectors. The resulting self-similar collapse must be matched to an exterior Vaidya spacetime and exhibits a monotonically decreasing Misner-Sharp mass due to outward energy transport. For both classes of solutions, the proper radius remains finite throughout the evolution, preventing the formation of shell-focusing singularities within the considered domain. The scalar field sector satisfies the null energy condition for the potentials studied, while the effective fluid sector exhibits violations of the null and strong energy conditions, indicating the emergence of effective exotic matter behavior.

It is the third of an infinite sequence of AF toric gravitational instantons that was proved to exist by Li and Sun, the first two being the Kerr and Chen--Teo instantons. It is also the first known example of a Ricci-flat gravitational instanton that is not Hermitian.

Result: The analysis yields a complex amplitude $ψ = \\sqrt{\\rho} e^{iS/\\hbar}$ whose squared modulus gives the spacetime density flow, and whose phase accumulates a topological contribution analogous to the Aharonov-Bohm effect when trajectories encircle or approach a black hole horizon.

Conclusion: This framework provides a novel semiclassical bridge between quantum mechanics and general relativity by endowing geodesic flows with wave-like properties, suggesting that gravitational analogs of quantum interference effects may be observable even in classical strong-field regimes like black hole spacetimes.

Abstract: In recent work, we have explored the novel possibility that the geodesic of a spacetime density flow in GR could be upgraded to an amplitude $ψ$ with density $|ψ|^2$. We show how this arises from generally-covariant quantum mechanics and the Klein-Gordon operator in a semiclassical analysis, and how it connects to the Raychaudhuri equations. In this setting, we demonstrate an Aharonov-Bohm type effect for the phase of $ψ$ in motion approaching a black hole.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: Starting from the covariant Maxwell equations in curved spacetime, the authors perform a first-order perturbative expansion around a flat background metric perturbed by a gravitational wave. They derive evolution equations for the electromagnetic field tensor $F_{\\mu\\nu}$ and the four-potential $A_\\mu$, impose the Lorenz gauge condition $\\nabla_\\mu A^\\mu = 0$, and analyze residual gauge freedom. The induced electric and magnetic fields are extracted from the perturbed $F_{\\mu\\nu}$, and the first-order correction to the electromagnetic energy-momentum tensor $T_{\\mu\\nu}^{(\\text{EM})}$ is computed. An explicit solution is obtained for the interaction between a monochromatic plane EM wave and a plane GW in transverse-traceless (TT) gauge.

Conclusion: The framework provides a consistent, gauge-aware, and fully relativistic description of GW-induced EM perturbations at linear order, enabling precise quantitative estimates of electromagnetic signatures from gravitational wave events. The small but non-negligible EM response ($\\sim 10^{-19}$) suggests that while direct detection remains challenging, such effects may be relevant in resonant or cumulative scenarios, or for future ultra-high-precision experiments.

该框架为引力波诱导的一阶电磁扰动提供了自洽、规范明确且完全相对论性的描述，可对引力波事件产生的电磁信号进行精确量化估计。尽管电磁响应微弱（$\\sim 10^{-19}$）使得直接探测极具挑战，但在共振、累积效应或未来超高精度实验中，此类效应仍可能具有可观测意义。

Abstract: We present a systematic theoretical framework for investigating first-order electromagnetic (EM) perturbations induced by gravitational waves (GWs). Beginning with the covariant Maxwell equations, we derive the complete first-order perturbation equations in terms of both the EM field tensor and the four-potential, demonstrating their equivalence alongside the residual gauge invariance under the Lorenz gauge condition. Furthermore, explicit first-order expressions for the induced electric and magnetic fields, as well as the associated EM energy-momentum tensor, are obtained. As an explicit illustration, we analytically evaluate the interaction between a plane EM wave and a GW within the transverse-traceless gauge. By demonstrating that the maximum modulus of the coupling coefficient is on the order of $10^2$, we quantitatively establish that a typical astrophysical GW with a dimensionless strain of $h_0 \sim 10^{-21}$ generates a first-order EM response on the order of $10^{-19}$ relative to the incident field amplitude.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: The authors construct SEOBNRv6EHM within the effective-one-body (EOB) framework, extending it to generic planar orbits. Validation includes mismatch computations and scattering-angle comparisons across a broad parameter space.\n\n

Result: SEOBNRv6EHM achieves waveform mismatches below or near $2\%$ for total masses $20–200\, \mathrm{M}_\odot$ and eccentricities up to $\sim 0.9$ at 14 periastron passages before merger, significantly outperforming previous models in the highly eccentric regime. Additionally, it runs 2–6 times faster than other state-of-the-art eccentric EOB models.\n\n
SEOBNRv6EHM 在总质量 $20–200\, \mathrm{M}_\odot$、偏心率高达 $\sim 0.9$（在并合前 14 个近星点处）的情况下，波形失配低于或接近 $2\%$，在高偏心率区域显著优于以往模型。对于准圆轨道和低偏心率系统，其精度与 SEOBNRv5 相当，同时稳健地扩展至散射和捕获情形。此外，其运行速度比其他先进偏心 EOB 模型快 2–6 倍。

Conclusion: SEOBNRv6EHM provides a fast, accurate, and comprehensive time-domain waveform model for gravitational-wave data analysis across a wide range of orbital eccentricities—including highly eccentric mergers and unbound scattering events—enabling unbiased parameter estimation and opening new avenues for probing dynamical formation channels of compact binaries with current and future GW detectors.\n\n
SEOBNRv6EHM 提供了一种快速、精确且全面的时域波形模型，适用于从低偏心率到高偏心率并合乃至非束缚散射事件的引力波数据分析，能够实现无偏参数估计，并为利用当前及未来引力波探测器研究致密双星的动力学形成通道开辟新途径。

Abstract: Accurate and computationally efficient waveform models are required to infer the parameters of compact binaries from their gravitational wave (GW) emission. Among these parameters, orbital eccentricity serves as a smoking gun for dynamical formation channels and must be accounted for to avoid systematic errors in GW analyses. Here, we present SEOBNRv6EHM, a time-domain, multipolar waveform model for binaries on generic planar orbits, calibrated to quasi-circular (QC) numerical-relativity (NR) simulations from the SXS collaboration. In addition to the dominant $(2,2)$ mode, the model provides the $(2,1)$, $(3,3)$, $(3,2)$, $(4,4)$, and $(4,3)$ multipoles for the full inspiral-merger-ringdown process of coalescing binaries, as well as for dynamical captures and scattering encounters. The model is built within the effective-one-body (EOB) framework, and it employs novel resummations of the radiation-reaction force and waveform modes. We validate its accuracy through comparisons against 592 QC, 319 eccentric, one dynamical-capture, and two scattering SXS NR waveforms, and through scattering-angle comparisons against 61 SXS NR simulations. For QC and small-eccentricity binaries, its accuracy is comparable to previous-generation SEOBNRv5 models. For highly eccentric systems, however, SEOBNRv6EHM attains unprecedented accuracy, with waveform mismatches remaining below or close to $ 2\% $ across the total mass range $ 20-200\, \mathrm{M}_\odot $ for eccentricities up to $\sim 0.9$ at 14 periastron passages before merger. Additionally, SEOBNRv6EHM achieves waveform-generation walltimes that are $ 2 - 6 $ times faster than other state-of-the-art EOB eccentric models, enabling efficient and accurate applications in GW astronomy.

  <summary>Details</summary>

The authors aim to develop and validate a more accurate and efficient eccentric waveform model (SEOBNRv6EHM) to enable reliable inference of eccentricity and related parameters from real GW observations.
\n\n
轨道偏心率是区分致密双星不同形成通道（例如孤立双星演化 vs. 致密星团中的动力学俘获）的关键判据。然而，现有的偏心波形模型存在不准确性，可能导致参数估计出现偏差。作者旨在开发并验证一个更精确、高效的偏心波形模型（SEOBNRv6EHM），以实现从真实引力波观测中可靠地推断偏心率及相关参数。

Method: The authors perform parameter-estimation (PE) analyses using the new SEOBNRv6EHM effective-one-body (EOB) waveform model, which handles generic planar eccentric binaries. They first validate the model against long, highly eccentric numerical-relativity (NR) waveforms as synthetic signals, comparing its performance with SEOBNRv5EHM and TEOBResumS-Dalí. Then, they apply SEOBNRv6EHM to 26 real GW events from O1–O4 observing runs, computing Bayes factors $\\log_{10} \\mathcal{B}^{\\text{EAS}}_{\\text{QCP}}$ to compare eccentric aligned-spin (EAS) models against quasi-circular precessing-spin (QCP) models. For five high-mass events, they further allow unbound initial conditions and compute $\\log_{10}\\mathcal{B}^{\\rm unbound}_{\\rm QCP}$.
\n\n
作者使用新的SEOBNRv6EHM有效单体（effective-one-body, EOB）波形模型进行参数估计（PE）分析，该模型适用于一般的平面偏心双星系统。他们首先以长时标、高偏心率的数值相对论（numerical-relativity, NR）波形作为合成信号来验证该模型，并与SEOBNRv5EHM和TEOBResumS-Dalí进行性能对比。随后，他们将SEOBNRv6EHM应用于O1–O4观测运行中的26个真实引力波事件，计算贝叶斯因子 $\\log_{10} \\mathcal{B}^{\\text{EAS}}_{\\text{QCP}}$，以比较偏心对齐自旋（eccentric aligned-spin, EAS）模型与准圆进动自旋（quasi-circular precessing-spin, QCP）模型。对于5个高质量事件，他们进一步允许无界初始条件，并计算 $\\log_{10}\\mathcal{B}^{\\rm unbound}_{\\rm QCP}$。

Result: SEOBNRv6EHM significantly reduces parameter estimation biases compared to SEOBNRv5EHM and TEOBResumS-Dalí when tested on synthetic NR signals. Applied to real data, five events show mild evidence for eccentricity ($\\log_{10} \\mathcal{B}^{\\text{EAS}}_{\\text{QCP}} > 0.5$). For three high-mass events (including GW190521), unbound (direct-capture) configurations yield $\\log_{10}\\mathcal{B}^{\\rm unbound}_{\\rm QCP} \\approx 0.2$–$0.6$, comparable to or slightly favored over bound eccentric or QCP models. However, the recovered unbound orbits are astrophysically unrealistic and statistically indistinguishable from highly eccentric bound orbits. SEOBNRv6EHM is also ~3× faster in PE than SEOBNRv5EHM.
\n\n
在合成NR信号测试中，SEOBNRv6EHM相比SEOBNRv5EHM和TEOBResumS-Dalí显著降低了参数估计偏差。在真实数据应用中，5个事件显示出轻微的偏心性证据（$\\log_{10} \\mathcal{B}^{\\text{EAS}}_{\\text{QCP}} > 0.5$）。对于3个高质量事件（包括GW190521），无界（直接俘获）构型的贝叶斯因子为 $\\log_{10}\\mathcal{B}^{\\rm unbound}_{\\rm QCP} \\approx 0.2$–$0.6$，与有界偏心或QCP模型相当或略占优势。然而，所恢复的无界轨道在天体物理上不现实，且在统计上无法与高偏心率的有界轨道明确区分。此外，SEOBNRv6EHM在参数估计中的计算速度比SEOBNRv5EHM快约3倍。

Conclusion: SEOBNRv6EHM represents a significant advance in eccentric waveform modeling, offering improved accuracy, reduced biases, and greater computational efficiency. It enables robust large-scale Bayesian inference of eccentricity in GW data. While some events show hints of eccentricity or even unbound origins, current evidence is insufficient to confirm dynamical capture scenarios, as unbound solutions are degenerate with highly eccentric bound orbits and lack astrophysical plausibility. Future improvements in waveform models and detector sensitivity are needed to definitively probe formation channels via eccentricity.
\n\n
SEOBNRv6EHM代表了偏心波形建模的重要进展，在精度、偏差控制和计算效率方面均有提升，使得在引力波数据中进行大规模贝叶斯偏心率推断成为可能。尽管某些事件显示出偏心性甚至无界起源的迹象，但目前的证据尚不足以确认动力学俘获场景，因为无界解与高偏心率有界轨道存在简并，且缺乏天体物理合理性。未来需通过改进波形模型和提升探测器灵敏度，才能通过偏心率明确揭示双星形成通道。

Abstract: Orbital eccentricity encodes key information about compact-binary formation channels and astrophysical environments, making it a critical target for gravitational-wave (GW) inference. We present parameter-estimation (PE) analyses of GWs from eccentric compact binaries with the SEOBNRv6EHM waveform model. Using long, highly eccentric numerical-relativity waveforms as synthetic signals, we compare parameter recovery across state-of-the-art eccentric models. We find that SEOBNRv5EHM and TEOBResumS-Dalí can yield biased estimates of eccentricity, masses, and spins in the most challenging configurations, while SEOBNRv6EHM significantly reduces these biases. Applying SEOBNRv6EHM to 26 GW events from the O1--O4 LIGO--Virgo--KAGRA observing runs -- including binary black hole, neutron-star--black-hole, and binary neutron-star mergers -- we identify five events with mild support for eccentricity over the quasi-circular precessing-spin hypothesis, with Bayes factors $\log_{10} \mathcal{B}^{\text{EAS}}_{\text{QCP}} > 0.5$. Since SEOBNRv6EHM is applicable to generic planar binaries, we reanalyze five high-mass events allowing for unbound initial conditions. For three of them -- including GW190521, previously claimed to originate from a dynamical capture -- a direct-capture configuration is comparable to, or marginally favored over, both the eccentric aligned-spin and quasi-circular precessing-spin hypotheses ($\log_{10}\mathcal{B}^{\rm unbound}_{\rm QCP} \approx 0.2$--$0.6$ for GW190521). The recovered configurations are, however, astrophysically unrealistic and cannot be confidently discriminated from highly eccentric bound orbits, so these results do not, by themselves, support an unbound origin for these events. SEOBNRv6EHM is approximately three times faster in PE analyses than SEOBNRv5EHM, while improving waveform accuracy, enabling efficient, large-scale GW inference with eccentric waveforms.

### [74] [Jacobson's thermodynamic approach to classical gravity applied to non-Riemannian geometries: remarks on the simplicity of Nature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.00422)
*Jhan N. Martinez,Jose F. Rodriguez-Ruiz,Yeinzon Rodriguez*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: The paper investigates the extension of Jacobson's thermodynamic derivation of gravity—originally formulated for Riemannian geometry—to non-Riemannian geometries (with torsion and/or non-metricity). It finds that General Relativity (from the Einstein-Hilbert action) is excluded in such generalized settings, except in the purely Riemannian limit. By combining Jacobson’s thermodynamic principle with the structural assumptions underlying Lanczos-Lovelock gravity, the authors identify a unique viable theory in the torsionful but metric-compatible case: Einstein-Hilbert gravity plus a quadratic torsion-vector term. However, this consistency breaks down in the presence of non-metricity or when using the canonical energy-momentum tensor.
\n\n
本文研究了将Jacobson最初为黎曼几何提出的引力热力学推导方法推广到非黎曼几何（含挠率和/或非度规性）的情形。研究发现，广义相对论（源自Einstein-Hilbert作用量）在这些广义情形下被排除，仅在纯黎曼极限下成立。通过将Jacobson的热力学原理与Lanczos-Lovelock引力理论的结构假设相结合，作者在含挠率但度规相容的情形下识别出一个唯一可行的理论：Einstein-Hilbert引力加上一个挠率矢量的二次项。然而，当存在非度规性或使用正则能量-动量张量时，这种一致性不再成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation stems from extending Ted Jacobson’s seminal 1995 result—that Einstein’s equations emerge from applying the first law of thermodynamics to local Rindler horizons—beyond Riemannian geometry. The authors aim to determine whether such a thermodynamic origin of gravity persists in more general spacetime geometries featuring torsion and/or non-metricity, and if so, which gravitational theories would be selected by this principle.
\n\n
动机源于将Ted Jacobson于1995年提出的开创性结果——即爱因斯坦方程可由对局域Rindler视界应用热力学第一定律导出——推广到黎曼几何之外的情形。作者旨在探究在包含挠率和/或非度规性的更一般时空几何中，引力是否仍具有此类热力学起源；若存在，则该原理会选出哪些引力理论。

Method: The authors generalize Jacobson’s thermodynamic argument to spacetimes with non-Riemannian connections (i.e., with torsion $T^\\lambda_{\\mu\
u}$ and possibly non-metricity $Q_{\\lambda\\mu\
u} = \
abla_\\lambda g_{\\mu\
u}$). They analyze the consistency between two criteria: (1) the field equations must follow from imposing Clausius relation $\\delta Q = T dS$ on local causal horizons (thermodynamic approach), and (2) the theory should satisfy the geometric and dimensional constraints that define Lanczos-Lovelock theories (e.g., second-order field equations, diffeomorphism invariance). They consider two definitions of the energy-momentum tensor: the metric (Hilbert) definition $T_{\\mu\
u} = -\\frac{2}{\\sqrt{-g}} \\frac{\\delta S_{\\text{matter}}}{\\delta g^{\\mu\
u}}$ and the canonical (Noether) definition involving variation w.r.t. the vielbein and connection.
\n\n
作者将Jacobson的热力学论证推广到具有非黎曼联络的时空（即含挠率 $T^\\lambda_{\\mu\
u}$ 和可能的非度规性 $Q_{\\lambda\\mu\
u} = \
abla_\\lambda g_{\\mu\
u}$）。他们分析两种判据之间的一致性：(1) 场方程必须通过对局域因果视界施加Clausius关系 $\\delta Q = T dS$ 得到（热力学方法）；(2) 理论应满足定义Lanczos-Lovelock理论的几何与维度约束（例如二阶场方程、微分同胚不变性）。他们考虑了能量-动量张量的两种定义：度规（Hilbert）定义 $T_{\\mu\
u} = -\\frac{2}{\\sqrt{-g}} \\frac{\\delta S_{\\text{matter}}}{\\delta g^{\\mu\
u}}$ 和正则（Noether）定义（涉及对vielbein和联络的变分）。

Result: In the case of metric-compatible connections with torsion (i.e., $Q_{\\lambda\\mu\
u}=0$ but $T^\\lambda_{\\mu\
u}\\neq0$), and when using the metric energy-momentum tensor, the only theory consistent with both Jacobson’s thermodynamic derivation and the Lanczos-Lovelock assumptions is described by the action:
$$
S = \\int d^4x \\sqrt{-g} \\left( R + \\alpha T_\\lambda T^\\lambda \\right),
$$
where $T_\\lambda = T^\\mu_{\\lambda\\mu}$ is the torsion vector and $\\alpha$ is a coupling constant. Notably, pure Einstein-Hilbert gravity ($\\alpha=0$) is not selected unless torsion vanishes identically. However, in the full non-Riemannian case (with non-metricity) or when using the canonical energy-momentum tensor, the two approaches yield mutually incompatible field equations, implying no consistent thermodynamic interpretation exists in those settings.
\n\n
在度规相容但含挠率的情形（即 $Q_{\\lambda\\mu\
u}=0$ 但 $T^\\lambda_{\\mu\
u}\\neq0$），并采用度规能量-动量张量时，唯一同时满足Jacobson热力学推导与Lanczos-Lovelock假设的理论由如下作用量描述：
$$
S = \\int d^4x \\sqrt{-g} \\left( R + \\alpha T_\\lambda T^\\lambda \\right),
$$
其中 $T_\\lambda = T^\\mu_{\\lambda\\mu}$ 为挠率矢量，$\\alpha$ 为耦合常数。值得注意的是，除非挠率恒为零，否则纯Einstein-Hilbert引力（$\\alpha=0$）不会被选出。然而，在完整的非黎曼情形（含非度规性）或使用正则能量-动量张量时，两种方法导出的场方程互不相容，表明这些情形下不存在一致的热力学诠释。

Conclusion: The thermodynamic origin of gravity, as proposed by Jacobson, does not uniquely select General Relativity in non-Riemannian geometries. Instead, in torsionful but metric-compatible spacetimes, it favors an Einstein-Cartan-type theory augmented by a specific quadratic torsion term. This suggests that the “simplest” theory (Einstein-Hilbert) may only be thermodynamically viable in the absence of torsion. Moreover, the inconsistency in the presence of non-metricity or with the canonical energy-momentum tensor indicates fundamental limitations of extending the thermodynamic paradigm beyond Riemannian geometry without additional physical input.
\n\n
Jacobson所提出的引力热力学起源在非黎曼几何中并不能唯一选出广义相对论。相反，在含挠率但度规相容的时空中，它倾向于一种带有特定挠率二次项的Einstein-Cartan型理论。这表明“最简单”的理论（Einstein-Hilbert）可能仅在无挠率时才具有热力学可行性。此外，在存在非度规性或使用正则能量-动量张量时出现的不一致性，揭示了在缺乏额外物理输入的情况下，将热力学范式推广到黎曼几何之外存在根本性限制。

Abstract: Three decades ago, Ted Jacobson surprised us with a very appealing approach to classical gravity. According to him, the gravitational field equations are the consequence of the first law of thermodynamics applied to a Rindler observer. Jacobson's approach being formulated for Riemannian geometries, we have wondered what its consequences would be for non-Riemannian geometries. The results of our quest have been particularly appealing: we have found that the theory that derives from the Einstein-Hilbert action, arguably ``the simplest one'', does not belong to the pool of gravitational theories available for Nature's selection (except in the Riemannian case). In the search of a unique alternative, we have considered the hypotheses employed in the formulation of the Lanczos-Lovelock theories of gravity. Together, the two approaches point towards the theory that derives from the Einstein-Hilbert action plus a term quadratic in the torsion vector as the one that would be selected by Nature in the non-Riemannian case without non metricity (when the energy-momentum tensor is identified as its metric version). The same strategy cannot be followed in the full non-Riemannian case (and in the previous case when the energy-momentum tensor is identified as its canonical version) as the two approaches are mutually inconsistent.

</details>


### [75] [Analytical Fluxes from Generic Schwarzschild Geodesics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13847)
*Majed Khalaf,Chris Kavanagh,Ofri Telem*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: The paper introduces an analytic method to compute gravitational-wave fluxes from bound Schwarzschild geodesics with arbitrary eccentricity by expanding Fourier coefficients in a Chebyshev basis, avoiding small-eccentricity approximations and achieving high accuracy even in strong-field regimes.
\n\n
本文提出了一种解析方法，通过将傅里叶系数展开为Chebyshev基，计算任意偏心率的束缚Schwarzschild测地线所辐射的引力波通量。该方法不依赖小偏心率展开，在强场区域仍能达到高精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing methods for computing gravitational-wave fluxes from eccentric orbits often rely on expansions in small eccentricity or numerical integration, limiting their applicability to moderate or weak-field regimes. A fully analytic, non-perturbative-in-eccentricity approach is needed for accurate modeling of extreme-mass-ratio inspirals (EMRIs) across the full parameter space of bound orbits.
\n\n
现有计算偏心轨道引力波通量的方法通常依赖于小偏心率展开或数值积分，限制了其在中等或弱场区域的应用。为了在全参数空间内精确建模极端质量比旋进（EMRIs），亟需一种完全解析且不对偏心率做微扰处理的方法。

Method: The authors expand the Fourier coefficients of the gravitational-wave flux in a Chebyshev polynomial basis. This expansion allows the coefficients to be expressed as linear combinations of Keplerian-like Fourier coefficients previously obtained via the Quantum Spectral Method. The approach avoids any expansion in eccentricity $e$, making it valid for arbitrary $0 \leq e < 1$. The method is implemented using a 15PN-expanded source term as input.
\n\n
作者将引力波通量的傅里叶系数展开为Chebyshev多项式基。该展开使得这些系数可表示为通过Quantum Spectral Method先前获得的类Keplerian傅里叶系数的线性组合。该方法避免了对偏心率 $e$ 的任何展开，因此适用于任意 $0 \leq e < 1$。文中使用15PN展开的源项作为输入实现该方法。

Result: For the orbit $(p,e)=(12.5,0.5)$, the method reproduces the total flux with relative accuracy $10^{-5}$. For the stronger-field case $(p,e)=(10,0.8)$, it achieves mode-by-mode weighted errors below $10^{-6}$ for dominant modes. These results validate the method’s high precision without requiring small-$e$ expansions.
\n\n
对于轨道 $(p,e)=(12.5,0.5)$，该方法复现总通量的相对精度达 $10^{-5}$；对于更强场情形 $(p,e)=(10,0.8)$，主导模式的加权逐模误差低于 $10^{-6}$。这些结果验证了该方法在无需小-$e$ 展开的情况下仍具有高精度。

Conclusion: The proposed Chebyshev-based analytic framework provides a robust and efficient route to frequency-domain gravitational-wave flux calculations for generic bound eccentric orbits in Schwarzschild spacetime, offering significant potential for improving waveform models in extreme-mass-ratio inspiral data analysis.
\n\n
所提出的基于Chebyshev的解析框架为Schwarzschild时空中一般束缚偏心轨道的频域引力波通量计算提供了稳健高效的方法，在改进极端质量比旋进（EMRI）波形模型和数据分析方面具有重要潜力。

Abstract: We present an analytic method for computing gravitational-wave fluxes from bound Schwarzschild geodesics with arbitrary eccentricity. Our approach systematically expands the Fourier coefficients of the emitted radiation in a Chebyshev basis, allowing them to be reduced to sums of Keplerian-like Fourier coefficients previously derived in the Quantum Spectral Method. Because the construction does not rely on a small-eccentricity expansion, it applies to a broad range of bound eccentric orbits. As an illustration, we implement the method using a $15$PN-expanded input and find that it reproduces the total flux for the case $(p,e)=(12.5,0.5)$ to relative accuracy $10^{-5}$, while for the stronger-field case $(p,e)=(10,0.8)$ it yields weighted mode-by-mode errors below $10^{-6}$ for the selected dominant modes analyzed. These results provide an analytic route to frequency-domain flux calculations relevant to extreme-mass-ratio inspirals.

</details>
